*bhagavat-saṅgi-saṅgasya martyānāṁ kim utāśiṣaḥ*

**“The value of a moment’s association with the devotee of he Lord cannot be compared to the attainment of heavenly planets or liberation from matter, and what to speak of worldly benedictions in the form of material prosperity, which are for those who are meant for death” (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **1.18.13).**

I remember when you were physically present with us, and we were in your association, how nice it was to observe your transcendental qualities. Each movement of yours was manifesting your spiritual position and was attracting us all to Kṛṣṇa. Your humility, your purity, your gravity, your attachment for the Lord all allowed us to get a glimpse of the presence of Kṛṣṇa. By the way you behaved it was obvious that you were always in front of Kṛṣṇa, and when we were with you we got the idea of what it meant to live with Kṛṣṇa.

I admired how respectful you were in your dealing with others, being conscious of the presence of Paramātmā in their hearts, and how you simultaneously exercised your authority as the bona fide external representative of the Supreme Lord.

Your complete dedication and your constant absorption in your service to the Lord, in whatever part of the world you were, inspired us to do the same without ever having to judge in our minds your example, because we had complete faith in you. This faith that you generated, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is the force that is moving us to convince others, and your example is still among us, illuminating the path of perfection. Simply by talking and hearing about you and remembering and practicing your instructions, you come alive and provide your soothing effect that relieves the miseries of material existence.

There is really no other way to repay you than distributing the same benefit to others, which was your most cherished desire.

The beautiful ISKCON movement, a branch of Lord Caitanya’s tree, that you have created is an oasis in the burning desert of Kali-yuga. In that oasis grow transcendental fruits like books, temples, Deities, *prasādam,* and wonderful devotees.

Let us therefore cooperate in Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s *saṅkīrtana* movement, which you have brought us, by contributing whatever we can for the glorification of Your Divine Grace and Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa, hoping in this way to serve and please you.

Your devotees at the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Ramaṇa Temple in Milan, Italy.

## Monterrey

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances on this auspicious day of your appearance in this world.

It has become a part of our life to write for you every year on this holy occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā day, because our life is yours and your are our life.

Twenty years ago, by authorizing us to get Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa *mūrtis* for Mexico, you gave us the opportunity to relish Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa *līlā*. At the same time you opened up to us the transcendental abode of Śrī Vṛndāvana-dhāma. In this way you made true the words of Śrīla Viśvanātha Cakravartī Ṭhākura:

*śrī-vigraharādhana-nitya-nānā-*  
*śṛṅgāra-tan-mandira-mārjanādau*  
*yuktasya bhaktāṁś ca niyuñjato ’pi*  
*vande guroḥ śrī-caraṇāravindam*

In this way it has been made easy for us to fix our minds on Kṛṣṇa, who is playing His flute in the forests of Vṛndāvana.

You have given everyone the chance to enter the highest relationship with the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Therefore you are our life, and our life is yours.

You are the bona fide representative of Śrīmatī Rādhikā, who is the best servitor in Śrī Vṛndāvana-dhāma. By Her mercy only can one attain Kṛṣṇa; therefore the devotees write Her divine name everywhere in Vṛndāvana.

My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, our prayer is this: Please make our hearts like Vṛndāvana in this way: Śrī Kṛṣṇa can come and live within. Therefore we have you in our heart, in our mind, and in our intelligence with some hope that Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa may one day manifest there by your grace. Therefore you are our life, and our life is yours.

Advaita Ācārya pacified Lord Caitanya by saying, “Wherever You are—there is Vṛndāvana.” You are a bona fide representative of Lord Caitanya. Thus by coming two times to Mexico, you have established Vṛndāvana here.

By your presence, by your books, by your temples, by your disciples, Vṛndāvana will always remain here, to give shelter to all conditioned souls. Therefore you have become our life, and our life is yours.

One who has received the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra* from you is on his way to Vṛndāvana. one who sees Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa *mūrtis* is on his way to Vṛndāvana. One who has seen you is on his way to Vṛndāvana. One who has heard from you is on his way to Vṛndāvana. One who has been in any way connected with your books—through reading, distributing, touching, etc.—is on his way to Vṛndāvana. One who has taken *prasādam* is on his way to Vṛndāvana. One who is living in your temple is in Vṛndāvana. Therefore our life is yours, and your life is ours.

By staying in Vṛndāvana in the Rādhā-Dāmodara temple, you have shown us how to prepare ourselves for preaching, and by opening temples all over the world you have made it easy to approach Vṛndāvana. By leaving Vṛndāvana you have shown the world how a Vrajavāsī preaches the glory of Vṛndāvana.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, implant in us the preaching mood of Vṛndāvana, which is identical with Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī’s eagerness to bring the conditioned souls closer to Kṛṣṇa.

Begging to remain your servants,  
Your devotees in Monterrey, Mexico.

## Moscow

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances thrown in the dust of your lotus feet.

Although we never had the good fortune to associate with you physically, still we are always associating with you through your divine instructions presented in your books, which are the source of our strength and determination. Therefore on this blessed day of your appearance we want to glorify Your Divine Grace for that matchless gift you gave us and the whole world.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, the embodiment of your divine personality is ISKCON, which you created to save the world from impersonalism in all its manifestations and from the ignorance, darkness, and evil born from it. For this reason ISKCON is endowed with great power to change the face of the world by purifying the hearts of the people. Hundreds of thousands of people are getting your mercy through your disciples and granddisciples. ISKCON is so powerful because it possesses spiritual purity. “Purity is the force,” you used to say, and therefore you sternly taught all of your disciples to strive for pure devotional service, of which you yourself are the embodiment. Because you rendered unalloyed devotional service to the lotus feet of your beloved spiritual master, you could cross over all difficulties and ignite a spiritual revolution. And inheriting this selfless spirit of purity of thought and action, the most elevated among your disciples became the channels of your mercy. They are fulfilling your mission, saving conditioned souls, teaching their disciples the principles of pure, unmotivated devotional service, instilling your mood in them. This transmission of your mercy makes your movement unconquerable and unique. You are not just a spiritual master: you are a carrier of Vedic culture, which you boldly and enthusiastically propagated among *mlecchas* and *yavanas*. And now, by your divine mercy, this divine culture is taking over the world.

You are the heart and soul of ISKCON. Inspired by your instructions and your personal example, thousands of *saṅkīrtana* devotees are distributing huge amounts of books. You invisibly pervade all the fields of ISKCON activities. The Food for Life program, which is successfully going on in our country, is a manifestation of your compassion. Whatever preaching activities you ever started were a success because Kṛṣṇa was always blessing all your initiatives. Therefore as long as we are unflinchingly dedicated to your instructions, and as long as we maintain selflessness and purity in serving you, our preaching endeavors will always be successful.

Therefore, throwing ourselves on the ground at your lotus feet, we beg you to allow us always and in all circumstances to remain devoted to your mission.

Your granddisciples in Moscow.

## Murāri-sevaka

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances.

You are the greatest *jīva* soul in modern history, extending for the last few thousand years. You are a pure lover of Kṛṣṇa, one of His dear, devoted, and intimate servants, personally sent and empowered by Him to execute His mission here. You have made it possible for everyone in the world to return home, back to the spiritual world, to the lotus feet of Lord Kṛṣṇa. You have given to modern man more real, substantial knowledge of the world, the living entity, God, the spiritual world, and the spiritual science of self-purification and God realization than anyone else in history. Your life and your books prove this. That is why, along with your order and mission, we want to distribute this knowledge widely so that everyone can benefit. Then they will know what a great, pure soul you are, a lover of the Lord and all His personal parts and parcels.

Thank you immensely for your kindness, and we also thank Kṛṣṇa for sending us His wonderful servant. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the savior of the world. The world is made up of people, and you have truly saved so many. Before you came, the world was in darkness, not knowing anything and acting in madness. But as the world gets to know you and Kṛṣṇa, it becomes enlightened, enlivened, orderly, and happy.

All praise, honor, and glory to His Divine Grace Śrīla Prabhupāda and to the one who sent him, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa! May we accept your mercy by sticking steadfastly to your lotus feet, by following in your footsteps, by acting according to your instructions.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Hare Kṛṣṇa!

The inmates and servants at the Murāri-sevaka farm community in Mulberry, Tennessee.

## Nagpur

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

This is an extremely auspicious and pleasing occasion for everyone because you appeared on this day. Without the presence of such a *mahātmā* as yourself, the entire world would be bereft of auspiciousness. It is said in all authentic revealed scriptures that actual auspiciousness begins for living entities when they come in touch with a pure devotee of Lord Kṛṣṇa. Your magnanimous contribution of Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the world will serve as the superlative example for the future *ācāryas* of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are the most dear disciple of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarsvatī Mahārāja because you embraced his ideas of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness on a worldwide basis. And you are the only one with sufficient broadness of heart and mind to endeavor and succeed in planting the Kṛṣṇa consciousness culture all over world, particularly in the Western world. While you were having your pastimes here, you were absorbed in spreading *sanātana-dharma* even though external circumstances were sometimes extremely unfavorable. In this way you displayed your firm faith in Kṛṣṇa and Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Mahārāja.

Even now it is due to your mercy and vision that Kṛṣṇa consciousness is spreading more and more despite many obstacles placed before us by illusion. Due to maintaining desires for sense gratification, we are foolishly trying to embrace Māyā, even when she kicks us strongly. We beg you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, please give us enough strength and intelligence to become some insignificant part of your dream to spread the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu in the Nagpur region. Nagpur is in Vidarbha. According to *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* Śrīmatī Rukmiṇī Devī was born in Vidarbha, in a village named Kaundilyapur, very near to Nagpur. The land of Vidarbha is blessed by Lord Kṛṣṇa. Lord Kṛṣṇa came to Kundaliṇī Pura (presently known as Kaundilyapur) to kidnap Rukmiṇī Devī. Lord Balarāma followed Kṛṣṇa with a big army to assist Him in a battle likely to take place with Śiśupāla and his demoniac associates, headed by Jarāsandha. Near Kaundilyapur is an important city known as Amarāvatī, founded by the elder brother of Rukmiṇī Devī. This region of Vidarbha is also blessed by Lord Rāma. While going south to Daṇḍakāranya, Lord Rāma spent some time there in Rāmateka, very near to Nagpur.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, bestow your mercy upon us so that we may be able to serve you. We have no shelter other than your lotus feet.

Your humble servants in Nagpur, India.

## New Dwārakā

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances; All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Please guide our intelligence in the path of light so that we can remind ourselves of our duties in your mission to relieve ourselves and the world from the darkness of nescience.

This year we are rededicating ourselves once again to simply follow your instructions—to bring Nārāyaṇa to as many people as possible in the form of your transcendental books—so that His consort, Lakṣmī, can be engaged in the Lord’s service. Because of the return of Lakṣmī, many innocent people are getting the opportunity to associate with the mercy of Kṛṣṇa. Our prayer is, “Please give us the strength to drown Los Angeles in your books!’

You instructed us, “One may live in a cave, in a cottage beside a river, in a palace, or in a big city like New York or London. In any case, a devotee can follow the instructions of his spiritual master and engage in devotional service by watering the Tulasī plant and chanting the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mantra*.”

Your instructions are ever present in your Hare Kṛṣṇa movement, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and we humbly beg for your mercy to allow us to follow your instructions “as they are” so that we can remain faithful servants of your New Dwārakā Dhām, engaging ourselves fully in your preaching mission by following the regulative principles strictly, worshiping the Tulasī plant, and continuously chanting the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra*.

We once again want to remind ourselves that there is no need to be overly concerned for material maintenance. As long as we do not want more than what is absolutely necessary and do not create artificial needs, and as long as we are alert in using everything only for Kṛṣṇa’s service, Kṛṣṇa will take care of all that we have and all that we need for His service.

We once again want to remind ourselves that the best way to get free of the clutches of Māyā is to concentrate our attention in the loving service of Śrī Śrī Rukmiṇī Dwārakādīśa. Even though provocation or misunderstanding may remain between one servant and another, our staunch faith in your instructions will protect us from becoming victims of material disruption.

We once again want to remind ourselves that the secret of success is to cling to your lotus feet so that in assisting you in the battle you have declared against Māyā we will always be protected by Kṛṣṇa. Since this age is especially fraught with dissension, whenever this arises between devotees please help us remember from what kind of ditch you picked all of us up. Please help us remember that the only qualification we have to scrutinize is if one is acting in Kṛṣṇa consciousness as far as one is able to do it.

We once again want to remind ourselves that Kṛṣṇa is not in need of our service at all but that if we utilize everything for His service, that is for our benefit only.

We once again want to remind ourselves that even though in our modern culture we were all taught not to become slaves, we did become slaves to so many abominable things—drugs, illicit sex, etc.—in the service of the senses. But when we agreed to become slaves of Your Divine Grace, we tasted what it is to be liberated from that slavery. Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please help us guard ourselves from again becoming the slaves of our senses. Let every talent be employed in your mission, and let us remain thankful at your door, guarding against any nonsense intruder and being satisfied with whatever you throw for us to eat for our sustenance.

We once again want to remind ourselves that we are neither renouncing nor enjoying, we are simply servitors of Your Divine Grace, the great servitor of Kṛṣṇa. Please help us follow this principle so that we will never be disturbed in any frightening condition offered by Māyā.

We once again want to remind ourselves of your instructions on gradually acquiring spiritual strength without failure by actually engaging in preaching work and not trying to assume advancement that tries to relish imitating great personalities.

We once again want to remind ourselves, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that

we do not need new lights, but proper vision,  
we do not need new heights, but fixed balance,  
we do not need new plans, but total dedication.

Prostrating ourselves at the lotus feet of Your Divine Grace, founder-*ācārya* of ISKCON and the golden age, we beg to remain

Your servants in New Dwārakā, Los Angeles.

## New Goloka

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Please accept our obeisances on this most auspicious day of your blessed appearance.

Here at New Goloka Dhāma we are aspiring to become your menial servants. We pray that this temple will be an oasis for all conditioned souls to receive your association through the simple and sublime process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness that you have taught us to follow. By your mercy, Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa have personally manifested Themselves here in Their beautiful forms of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Golokānanda and are attracting many to take shelter at Their lotus feet. By the distribution of Their *prasādam* we are trying to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness throughout the area.

Let us always keep firm faith in your programs of book distribution, *prasādam* distribution, and Deity worship, for in that way we will be assured that you will always live with us here at New Goloka Dhāma. We are all dependent on your instructions to guide us out of this material world and attract us back to Kṛṣṇaloka. It is impossible to thank you for everything you have given us and will give us, for your instructions are ever unfolding and always available to those who wish to reciprocate with you through your books, tapes, and devotees.

Please forgive us for our inability to serve you properly, and please guide us so that we may grow and help others in a way that is pleasing to you.

Your servants at New Goloka Dhāma, in Hillsborough, North Carolina.

## New Govardhana

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances.

The whole world is obsessed with questions. They range from the birds’ puzzling over where the worms are, to the *jñānīs’* speculations about the Absolute, to a sincere soul’s asking “What is the nature of God?” and “Why must I suffer and die?”.

Imperfect questions and imperfect answers are the hallmarks of our modern civilization, and this is exemplified by an accident that occurred here a couple of years ago, a little north of New Govardhana. One evening some young men were walking on a road that runs along the cliffs above the ocean. Being unfamiliar with the area, they thought they could get to the beach by climbing down the bluffs. It was dark, and when one of them tried to get to the beach, he slipped, slid off the cliff, and fell fifty feet to the hard sand below. His companions, although unable to see what had happened, knew their friend was in trouble. In their attempt to help, two more fell and met Yamarāja on the sand below.

These men had an imperfect question, about how to get to the beach, and it was coupled with an imperfect answer of attempting to climb down a sheer cliff.

By your grace, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we've been given the right questions, the right answers, and a desire to make the understanding of these the goal of our lives. You have flooded us with instructions in your books, letters, conversations, and lectures about how to attain perfection. But just theoretically knowing these instructions or being able to parrot them mechanically is not enough. We need something more—we need realization.

Knowledge without realization is useless, like a rich man with amnesia who lives in an impoverished condition unnecessarily. Or consider the young beachgoers again: even if they were given the knowledge (*jñāna*) about how to safely reach the sand, without concomitant action (*vijñāna*) the same tragic result would have occurred.

Here at New Govardhana we are striving to more closely align ourselves with your instructions because we know that properly following them, with the associated emphasis on preaching, is the key that unlocks the door to unimagined benefit for ourselves and the world.

After Sanātana Gosvāmī was instructed by Lord Caitanya, Sanātana prayed that the Lord’s teachings would actually manifest in his heart. Unless one is blessed by the spiritual master, the teachings will not be fully understood. So, Śrīla Prabhupāda, in a mood of wanting only to please you, the *ācāryas,* and the Supreme Lord, Kṛṣṇa, we pray for the blessing that we can properly carry out your divine mission.

Your aspiring servants and grandservants at New Govardhana Dhāma, San Diego, California

## New Jagannātha Purī

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

You are the actual savior of the modern world, rescuing it from its suicidal so-called civilization. In the *Bhaviṣya Purāṇa* Lord Kṛṣṇa tells Gaṅgā-devī that for the first five thousand years of Kali-yuga the process of purification will be to bathe in her holy waters, and for the next ten thousand years the process of purification will be *saṅkīrtana*. It cannot be coincidental that Your Divine Grace appeared on this planet just before the beginning of this ten-thousand-year period, initiating worldwide *saṅkīrtana*.

Thank you for giving us the topmost service to Śrī-Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa—distributing your books. All we need is your books—the knowledge in them, the practice of devotional service explained in them, and the topmost *sādhana* of distributing them.

Some less intelligent devotees may think that *paramahaṁsas* are only *bābājīs* who spend their days simply meditating on the eightfold daily pastimes of Śrī-Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa, and who then enter those pastimes in a spiritual body to assist in the loving affairs of the Divine Couple. They do not realize that Your Divine Grace, the topmost *paramahaṁsa,* has taught us that the best service we can render to the transcendental debauchee is to bring Him more souls to love Him—and the way we can do that is by giving them your books.

We cannot glorify Your Divine Grace and your books adequately. As we work together in love and trust, remembering that we are all your servants and that our most important work is distributing your books, we will advance steadily in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We will become happy as your books are distributed, for the world will become happy. Thank you for coming to us and giving us yourself, your personal example of self-sacrifice, humility, loving concern for all living entities, and your books. We will remain eternally indebted to You.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Your fallen servants at New Jagannātha Purī Dhāma, Berkeley, California.

## New Māyāpur

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances.

You blessed the earth in all directions as you relentlessly traveled and established your glorious ISKCON. Hardly anyone outside of India had ever heard about Kṛṣṇa, but in a few years of bold and powerful preaching you made Kṛṣṇa a household word. Only a handful of scholars had heard about Vaiṣṇavism, but you brought the message of Gaurāṅga Mahāprabhu to the distant shores of the Western world and introduced everyone to this most sublime path.

All glories to your lotus feet, meditated upon constantly by your true disciples, who, on your order, push vigorously forward the broadcasting of Kṛṣṇa’s glories as you have described them in your books for the benefit of suffering, ignorant mankind.

A person is pushed to enjoy this material world, and as he enjoys it, victimized by his mind and senses, he cannot understand its real nature. So *tapasya,* voluntarily giving up sense enjoyment, is necessary to make him sober up from his intoxicated state and realize that this world is a temporary abode filled with various miseries. You boldly came and accomplished the greatest miracle: to convince gross sense enjoyers to give up their nasty habits and perform austerity, the last thing a Westerner wants to hear about. And as they flocked to your lotus feet, you turned them into devotees, as a powerful alchemist turns iron into gold.

All glories to your lotus feet, which came all the way to this New Māyāpur Dhāma and blessed it with your very steps. You sat under the great evergreen tree, glowing with divine effulgence, and spoke as we listened, spellbound: “To think about Kṛṣṇa constantly, as the Pāṇḍavas did—is there any difficulty?” Being the very embodiment of the *“man-manā bhava mad-bhakto”* verse, you communicated to us your deep, tranquil conviction. As you repeated again and again the same point, “Is there any difficulty?” we all answered, “No, Śrīla Prabhupāda.” And in your presence there was no difficulty. We could not see Kṛṣṇa directly, but you were there, *kṛṣṇa-kṛpānanda-mūrti,* the blissful personification of Kṛṣṇa’s mercy. Verses of the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* took their full, clear meaning in your presence: “In the form of Guru, the Lord comes to deliver the fallen souls,” or “Kṛṣṇa appears as the best of the devotees.”

All glories to your lotus feet, where you gave shelter to anyone approaching you, even if with meager sincerity. As the most noble and magnanimous personality, you simply saw the desire, however small, to surrender to Kṛṣṇa and serve Him, and you overlooked our countless faults. Children want candies and toys, whereas grown-ups just want to find some solace and forget that this world is not paradise but a valley of tears. You accepted as your children all those who came forward. All glories to your lotus feet, the dust of which we pray to receive on our heads in every birth we take.

May your glorious lotus feet always shine in our hearts and in the hearts of all your disciples and followers, dispelling all darkness.

Your servants at the New Māyāpur farm in France.

## New Ramaṇ-reti

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful, humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

Like my Godbrothers before me, I would like to present a wonderful descriptive narrative worthy of Your Divine Grace’s special position as the dearmost representative of the Supreme Personality of Godhead.

This narrative would proclaim your unique contributions and historical accomplishments and would please the devotees and amaze the general mass of people. It would also enumerate and elaborate upon the 26 jewellike qualities of a *sadhu* that you fully manifested so that even fools like me could see them—qualities like tolerance, mercy, and poetic ability, such as when you glorified His Divine Grace Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Goswami Prabhupāda with your famous poem that correctly identified the essence of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta’s mission: “Absolute is sentient, thou has proved. Impersonal calamity thou hast removed”.

Unfortunately, I can hardly contemplate, much less execute, anything worthy of printing.

The only solace I have is that by recalling memories of you from the normally vapid recesses of my mind to the forefront of my consciousness (where they should be), I am renewed by those sentiments born of separation from you, which well up uncontrollably in my heart and appear for a moment like love and devotion but which in the heart /mind of a harlequin such as me must surely be contrived. Who is there that can be more illusioned than I? In such a nebulous state I can only beg to be meaningfully engaged in your loving service, birth after birth, till I am rectified to your satisfaction. May you always remain our “ever well wisher.”

Your aspiring servants in New Ramaṇ-reti, Alachua, Florida.  
(Written by Hamsarupa dasa)

## Toronto

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*akṣṇoḥ phalaṁ tvādṛśa-dārśanaṁ hi*  
*tanoḥ phalaṁ tvādṛśa-gātra-saṅgaḥ*  
*jihvā-phalaṁ tvādṛśa-kīrtanaṁ hi*  
*sudurlabhā bhāgavatā hi loke*

**“O pure devotee, the purpose of the eyes is to see you. The purpose of the touch is to touch your body, the purpose of the tongue is to glorify you, because in this world it is very difficult to find a pure devotee like you.”**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your holy feet, which are the only shelter for those sincere souls wishing to get free from this ocean of misery.

No matter how much we serve and glorify you, our debt towards you is eternal—we cannot repay it.

We know, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that flowers and beautiful words alone will not please you. You have great hope for this movement, and the only way we find to partially reciprocate with you for what you gave us is to serve ISKCON with full determination and courage.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, keep giving us the privilege of serving you and your instructions.

All glories to your Appearance day!  
All glories to your instructions!  
All glories to ISKCON!

Your servants at New Remuṇa Dhāma, Toronto, Canada.

## New Tālavana

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble, prostrated obeisances in the dust of your divine feet.

Please allow us to continue engaging in your powerful *saṅkīrtana-yajña*. Our efforts are a very small aspect of your mission. Some *prabhus* would question its usefulness. We are like the squirrel or spider that assisted Lord Rāmacandra in building His bridge to Laṅkā.

Although we are purchasing land, worshiping you and your beautiful Deities, raising families, performing devotional service, protecting some cows, etc., we are in a part of America that has negative connotations as a Bible belt region characterized by bigotry, fundamentalism, poverty, racism, etc. Even within our Society persons sometimes dwell on the faults of material existence to the point that the positive becomes diminished. Ultimately, the positive is *bhakti-yoga*. Our *sampradāya* is delivering people everywhere. Here is no exception. Consequently, as the years roll on, we continue to offer our humble fruits to you. Please accept these fruits produced by your loving disciples.

We are certainly not very important, but we are part and parcel of your gigantic *saṅkīrtana* movement. Therefore we have become extremely fortunate in having an unlimited opportunity to receive and distribute true happiness in the form of your *saṅkīrtana* movement.

Just as the Supreme Personality of Godhead is most interested in the pure love and devotion of His unlimited energies, so are you. In fact, the *jīvātmā* serves the Paramātmā by purely serving the Lord’s devotees.

Although we may be an insignificant part of your society, your interest is in our humble, tolerant, and prideless attempts to cooperate with and serve your mission. Just as the tiny creatures’ service in Rāma-līlā was appreciated by Lord Rāma, similarly, whatever part of our service is pure and loving is appreciated and loved by you, thus pleasing the Supreme Personality of Godhead and resulting in our enlightenment and the enlightenment of our subordinates.

Please forgive all the faults that cover our services. We plead with all your disciples, their disciples, and your followers to forgive our innumerable faults. Please continue to give us the opportunity to exclusively engage in Śrīla Prabhupāda’s service. That will make our life perfect.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Whatever spiritual *śakti* we possess is due to you. We fall again and again in the dust of your divine lotus feet and continuously beg for the nectar of your transcendental loving service.

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your servants at the New Tālavana farm community in Carriere, Mississippi.

## New Vrajamandala

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our prostrated obeisances at your lotus feet.

We are souls who are lost, going around without sense in the universe of Māyā. We are bewildered by the illusory energy of the Lord, and we are tired from our attempts at getting some substantial happiness in this world of craziness. However, due to your transcendental devotional service to Guru and Gaurāṅga, so many hopeless souls like ourselves have received the merciful blessings of the Supreme Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu. As Lord Gaurāṅga Mahāprabhu Himself explains in the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta:*

“That person who is able to convince even the *mlecchas* (meat-eaters) about devotional service to Kṛṣṇa is a great personality. Therefore, who can calculate the value of his Vaiṣṇavism? This is an extremely difficult thing to do, and whoever does it is situated at the highest level of love for Kṛṣṇa.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you were the person who brought hope and light to our poor lives. You worked tirelessly to firmly establish Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world, and because of your kindness you have expertly established the basis for practicing spiritual life in your wonderful translations and Bhaktivedanta purports of the most important literature for the upliftment of all human beings: the *Bhagavad-gītā,* the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* and the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta*. In them you have given us the major part of your treasure. Simply by knowing this transcendental literature, by practicing it, and by preaching it we can also know your heart and become attached to your *saṅkīrtana* movement and in this way attain the most exalted goal of existence, pure love for Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the Lord of Vṛndavāna.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you also have empowered all your followers so that they can carry out your instructions and expand this miraculous International Society for Krishna Consciousness. By your desire this is being done by the translation and distribution of your books, by establishing temples and Deities to teach the technique of spiritual life, by preaching in different fields and in different ways according to the nature and capacity of everyone, by distributing big amounts of *prasādam,* by chanting the holy name, etc. In this way you have created a new world and a new life for all the souls who are sincere about obtaining the truth. Your glories cannot be described properly in a simple offering, but we are trying to please you through our attempt to worship you in a pure and worthy way in spite of all our disqualifications.

You are the merciful agent of the Lord, and we are sure that through you all our efforts in devotional service can attain perfection simply if you accept them.

We would like to take this opportunity on this auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā to ask you for a benediction. First, we very much cherish the desire of remaining always in the association of devotees, engaged unceasingly in the activities of devotional service. Please help us improve our relations considerably so that we can create a very united and strong community where there are no misunderstandings, dissatisfaction, or quarrels. Second, we are far from perfectly following your instructions, and because of that there are some programs remain somewhat undeveloped and difficult. So please give us the necessary vision and energy to satisfy your desires. And in the meantime, please help us to always remain tolerant and humble so that we can cooperate with one another and appreciate your wonderful presence in all circumstances.

May our dedication to devotional service never get spoiled, and may our dedication to Kṛṣṇa always increase.

Begging forgiveness for all our offenses and hoping that you overlook all our defects, we remain  
Your servants in New Vrajamandala, Brihuega, Spain.

## New York

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our heartfelt obeisances. All glories to your divine lotus feet, which have graced both the East Side and West Side of Manhattan Isle, as well as all other major areas in the Eastern and Western world.

On this most auspicious day, we would like to bring a particular incident to your attention. It transpired many, many years ago, and it involves one of your eternal associates. The reason this story is significant, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is that it shows, beyond the shadow of a doubt, that your were greatly successful in your mission, and that you knew that your success would come to pass.

Once, in the time of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, your beloved Guru Mahārāja, there was a young boy named Dol Govinda Shastri. We doubt that this was his name back then, but it’s his name now. Anyway, he was a young boy, and he was trained in philosophy and Sanskrit by Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Mahārāja. He became quite a Sanskritist, and he was asked by Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta to train all of the Gauḍīya Maṭha men in the Sanskrit language. At one point, Dol Govinda was giving lessons in the same building where “Abhay Babu”—as you were referred to when a householder—was living. You remember, Śrīla Prabhupāda—you would sit on the steps of the building and chant *japa* with great concentration.

One day, as Dol Govinda was coming down the steps, he said, “Abhay Babu? The scriptures say that Mahāprabhu’s message will travel to every town and village of the world. How is it possible?”

“Are you doubting the words of the scriptures?” you asked this well-meaning *paṇḍita.*

“No! No!” Dol Govinda quickly responded. “I just can’t understand how it will actually happen.”

“Any fool disciple of our beloved Guru Mahārāja will do it,” you told him, “but you will not.”

This answer confused Dol Govinda. You were speaking with him, of course, in your native Bengali. But you used the plural when you told him that “you will not \[do it\].” In other words, you said, “You all will not do it.” What could you have possibly meant? “Any fool disciple will do it, but you all will not.” It was a strange way to say that Dol Govinda himself was not going to accomplish the task at hand. And if you were speaking about all of the disciples, properly using the plural form of the word, then who in fact would spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness to every town and village of the world?

Years passed. It was the mid-1970’s, and Dol Govinda was walking in Cuttack. He had never heard of the Hare Kṛṣṇa movement, but in the distance he saw a white man with red hair who was dressed like a perfect Vaiṣṇava, with robes and *japa-mālā*. He ran up to the young man and said, “Who are you?” The devotee then respond, “I am Bhāgavat dāsa, a disciple of His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda.”

“Who is that?” Dol Govinda asked.

Bhāgavat pulled out a picture of you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and upon seeing it Dol Govinda exclaimed, “Abhay Babu! You did it! You did it!”

So the story is quite interesting: You humbly told him that any fool disciple will do it—you were actually, in all humility, referring to yourself. In your own way, you told Dol Govinda, many years before it happened, that all the disciples of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta would not be doing it. The only disciple who remained to do it was Your Divine Grace. And you did it.

And you did it with character and panache! You didn’t preach merely to old men and women, like other swamis and gurus who came to America. You preached to the young and the wild. The youth, with their drugs, free sex, and rock-’n’-roll. You didn’t go to London—at least this wasn’t your first stop—but you came instead to New York. You came to the Rotten Apple, and not only did you purify this apple, but you offered it to Kṛṣṇa. You’ve made it *mahā-prasādam*! Your chanting of the *mahā-mantra* in Tompkins Square Park reverberated right to the core of this apple, forcing its many worms to dance in ecstasy. For this we are grateful, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and although we can never repay you, we will at least take this opportunity to make a vow: we will try to honor you by extending the treasure you have bestowed upon us to others, until all of New York has tasted the sweet juice of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Your unworthy servants in New York City.  
(written by Satyarāja dāsa)

## Panama

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful obeisances at your lotus feet on this glorious day of Vyāsa-pūjā.

Kṛṣṇa—the Supreme Personality of Godhead, the original spiritual master, the supreme authority—and his bona fide representative are the only persons who can communicate the message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness as it is, without any change. That same Lord says in *Bhagavad-gītā* (4.34): “Just try to learn the truth by approaching a spiritual master. Inquire from him submissively and render service unto him. The self-realized souls can impart knowledge unto you because they have seen the truth.”

O divine master, Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is only by your causeless mercy that we are now on our way out of the long sleeping condition of life whose principal characteristic is the hard struggle for existence in trying to enjoy the three modes of nature apart from the Lord.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, every day around the world your lotus feet are being worshiped and glorified by your faithful devotees. Please, Gurudeva, let me follow in their footsteps. On this wonderful day of Vyāsa-pūjā we have the great benediction of being able to develop a better consciousness of you and at the same time to be grateful for your ocean of mercy.

Please accept our humble homage of offering at your lotus feet. We pray to remain eternally your servants, carrying out your instructions within ISKCON.

Your lowly servants and grandsons in Panama.

## Paris

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Throughout your whole life, you never left the association of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, because you always remembered Him. By the transcendental and mystical arrangement of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, you came to the West at a moment, in a context, where the whole human society went through a profound change. Later we realized that all this was an arrangement of Kṛṣṇa. Kṛṣṇa sent you into this specific situation, this specific social milieu, simply so that you could begin to propagate the *yuga-dharma* on a worldwide scale. Historians, philosophers, scientists, seekers of all kinds will one day realize that your appearance had been planned by the Supreme Lord personally. O Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is said that no amount of money can pay back the debt we owe the spiritual master. We, and humanity at large, have a debt that it is impossible to pay back with any amount of this world’s wealth, and this debt we owe to you. The only way we can hope to please you is by remembering and putting into practice all the instructions you left for the welfare of humanity. You worked so hard, non-stop, to nurture and build your ISKCON society. In addition you left us your Bhaktivedanta purports, which are already famous and which will become more and more renowned, for in them we can find objective, practical instructions in every field, spiritual and material, giving to mankind definite solutions to each and every problem imaginable. Exactly twenty years ago, on August 10, 1973, you installed Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Parisīśvara. You left very important instructions for the preaching in France. You always desired that the movement in France be established in a very organized fashion so that we could attract the confidence and trust of the whole society. This year, on the occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā and the commemoration of the twentieth anniversary of the installation of Rādhā-Parisīśvara, we have the opportunity to remember and to again begin to put into practice your very important instructions for Their service. In *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (1.15.28-29), we find the description of how Arjuna was feeling completely depressed due to separation from Kṛṣṇa, and how as soon as Arjuna began to remember the instructions he had received from Śrī Kṛṣṇa, he regained all his power, determination, and enthusiasm. Sūta Gosvāmī explains:

*evaṁ cintavato jiṣṇoḥ kṛṣṇa-pāda-saroruham*  
*sauhārdenātigāḍhena śāntāsīd vimalā matiḥ*  
*vāsudevāṅghry-anudhyāna-paribṛṁhita-raṁhasā*  
*bhaktyā nirmathitāśeṣa-kaṣāya-dhiṣaṇo ‘rjunaḥ*

**“Sūta Gosvāmī said: Thus being deeply absorbed in thinking of the instructions of the Lord, which were imparted in the great intimacy of friendship, and in thinking of His lotus feet, Arjuna’s mind became pacified and free from all material contamination. Arjuna’s constant remembrance of the lotus feet of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa rapidly increased his devotion, and as a result all the trash in his thoughts subsided.”**

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, by following in the footsteps of Arjuna, if we can constantly keep your instructions in our memory and put them into practice, surely we will succeed in our preaching work in France, for your satisfaction. Finally, by your mercy we have opened a preaching center in Paris this year. At the same time, we have re-established the Food for Life program in France under the name of “Repas Hare Kṛṣṇa.” On this holy day of your appearance, we humbly pray to have the determination and force to continue to establish your movement in France very solidly, as you always desired. We humbly beg you to give us the force to be able to re-install Rādhā-Parisīśvara in Paris itself, as you did exactly twenty years ago. We know that all the power and spiritual strength to succeed in doing this is to be found in your instructions. On this holy day of your appearance, we pray that you will give us your protection so that we will be able to remember and to put into practice your transcendental instructions. You have given us the secret: “If you continue to distribute my books and to set a personal example in your daily life, in your behavior, if you continue to make new devotees, the movement will always continue to expand; automatically it will be established in society and in the whole world.” Therefore we, your disciples and followers from the Paris temple, wish to give you every year on the day of your Vyāsa-pūjā the gift of expanding the movement in Paris and throughout France, because your only desire is to see all the living entities engaged in chanting the *mahā-mantra,* for by this practice an imprisoned living entity can attain liberation by awakening his original identity, his eternal relation with the Supreme Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

*titikṣavaḥ kāruṇikaḥ suhṛdaḥ sarva-dehinām*  
*ajāta-śatravaḥ śāntāḥ sādhavaḥ sādhu-bhūṣaṇāḥ*

**“The symptoms of a** ***sādhu*** **are that he is tolerant, merciful, and friendly to all living entities. He has no enemies, he is peaceful, he abides by the scriptures, and all his characteristics are sublime” (*****Bhāg.*** **3.25.21).**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, if we wish to humbly continue to work as seriously as possible to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness in all areas and at all levels here in Paris and in all of France, it is because we know that the only thing that can satisfy your heart is to see all the living beings of the universe engaged in the devotional service of the Supreme Lord.

Your humble servants in Paris, France.

## Perth

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

O dear Prabhupāda, when will I, by your grace, enter the eternal pastimes of our Lord?

The time is passing like a strong current of a river. And I feel a great distance does separate us.

O most wonderful of all persons, when will I be blessed to serve you with an aching heart which cries out for your mercy?

O Lord and master, let this heart, which is filled with all nasty things, come to understand that my greatest fortune has been found in worshiping your lotus feet.

O greatest of all fathers, be merciful to your obstinate son, who still desires to avoid worldly pains and enjoy worldly delights. When will I give up my insensitivity, the mentality of a grossly haunted madman, and learn by your divine grace how to feel loving compassion for all living entities?

O Gurudeva, I came into this world in total ignorance, but by your divine grace you have brought an ocean of knowledge. So as this knowledge frees my consciousness from all false designations, let me by your unbounded mercy always understand my lowly position, never thinking, “Now I am something.” Rather, let me please you by pleasing your servants for all eternity.

Your servants in Perth, Australia.  
(written by Hṛṣīkeśa dāsa)

## Philadelphia

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

No offense meant to my dear Godbrothers and Godsisters, but as the years go on, Śrīla Prabhupāda, I realize what a “BUNCH” we are that you have for disciples. Sometimes I have imagined all the generations of devotees assembling for some big event in the spiritual sky—grouping together in a kind of spiritual reunion. Prominent among the devotees are the Vaiṣṇavas of the Brāhma-sampradāya … and amongst them, the members of the Gauḍīya- sampradāya especially catch the attention: dancing and singing Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare. As I imagine these devotees—rows upon rows—I detect first a stir, then a murmur, then animated conversation crescendoing into a roar of comments as Your Divine Grace walks into the crowd with your band of disciples. Your “boys and girls,” as you called us. As you walk with your head held high we hear, “Who is that *sādhu*? Who is that saintly soul surrounded by a band of strange-looking men and women? See the ones I mean? Some of them in *dhotīs* and *sārīs*—a few have *tilaka* and neckbeads—*japa-mālā*. But for the most part they all seem a little “out of order” to me. Just look at them! I hear they cook pizza, enchiladas, veggie burgers, and carob chip cookies as well as rice, *dāhl* and *sabjī* for Kṛṣṇa. Some of them are rowdy and they sometimes push and shove one another. Look! Their socks don’t always match!!!”

Imagining this spectacle, I see you smiling. I am amazed: you are proud of us. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you understood Kṛṣṇa’s heart and brought us to His lotus feet although we were misfits, the most fallen. (“Actually, none of my disciples are first class,” you said.) You are the personification of Kṛṣṇa’s mercy and the embodiment of Lord Caitanya’s process. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for accepting us and loving us as we were: you have dusted us off with the holy name so that we may shine like moons reflecting the Kṛṣṇa sun.

*jaya śrī-caitanyacandrera bhakta candra-gaṇa*  
*sabara prema-jyotsnāya ujjvala tri-bhuvana*  
*(Cc. Ādi 13.5)*

A new generation of devotees is here now. They are just coming up, much as the crocuses in the late winter and early spring push up through that last blanket of snow. We all can see how much more qualified they are to learn and preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Kṛṣṇa has sent them so we can push on your ISKCON with their help. Because they are respectful, they give us all credit, but we see, Śrīla Prabhupāda, the hand of Kṛṣṇa. Out of His love for you, His dearmost devotee, Kṛṣṇa has managed to cover over our shortcomings and our faults. You gave us your unconditional love, and you actually appreciate our meager and faulty efforts to serve you. Our only claim to achievement is that we held it together with blue tac, masking tape, and faith in you. You will be judged by your ISKCON, and you will be judged by your disciples. Kṛṣṇa sends these new *bhaktas* to bring glory to your ISKCON, your disciples, but ultimately to you, Your Divine Grace. There is nothing higher than Kṛṣṇa’s love for His devotee. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are beautiful.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

Your servants in Philadelphia, Pennsylvania.  
(written by Saudāmaṇi-devī dāsī)

## Potomac

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

All glories to the auspicious appearance day of Your Divine Grace, and kindly accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Fortunately there are two types of devotees in this world, the *bhajanānandīs* and the *goṣṭhy-ānandīs*. For the fallen souls wandering in this country, lost in nescience, the mercy flowing from your association is the only hope. You had the perfect spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī, and the perfect place to worship, Śrī Vṛndāvana. You could have very easily attained Lord Kṛṣṇa there in your advanced age by your devotional *bhajana*. However, following the footsteps and order of your spiritual master, you struggled very hard up to the last moment of your life for others’ benefit. You gave us the perfect example of a *parivrājakācārya* preacher engaged in the service of all the lost souls. By your instructions you have also instilled this mood in your disciples and followers. You once said to devotees who were anxious to retire for more hearing and chanting, “Work now, *samādhi* later.” In this way you have left us a worldwide movement as our workplace.

In devotional service the means and the end are the same. We perform devotional service in or to get more devotional service, and ultimately Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī is arranging for Lord Kṛṣṇa to accept all the service, whether it be performed in the spiritual world, Goloka Vṛndāvana, or here on the mundane plane. Lord Kṛṣṇa is the enjoyer of all our activities.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we know that wherever you are under the order of Lord Kṛṣṇa you are absorbed in the most sublime service to their Lordships. We pray on this special day for the determination to carry on our devotional service with the same concentration, realizing that this is the secret of ever feeling connected to Your Divine Grace in all circumstances, at all times. Here in the capital of the United States we have our work cut out for us. Although a new president was elected this year, the same old problems exist—birth, death, old age, and disease—with the threefold miseries increasing daily. Please continue to shower your mercy on your servants so that we can increase our service to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Madana-mohana and bring many of the lost *jīvas* back to Their shelter.

Your humble servants in Potomac, Maryland, and throughout the Washington, D.C., area.  
(written by Śaṣṭhīvara dāsa)

## Poznan

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your lotus feet, which are the only shelter for so many living entities!

“Even though the people of the world have forgotten God and may say that God is dead, this is not a fact. One can understand God when one takes to the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement, and thus one can be happy” (*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 8.1.13).

Your words in your Bhaktivedanta purports in *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* are so splendid. Simple but sublime, carrying the deepest meaning of our existence, they give us a chance to go back home, back to Godhead. You would always emphasize how important it is to read them, to meditate on them, and to live by them. In this way you taught us how to overcome our atheistic nature, which makes us forget who we really are. In a few sentences you would express the highest philosophical truths, as well as compassion for all of us drowning in the dead of Māyā’s night. Always vigilant, you never let your disciples and future readers of your books deviate from real knowledge. Whoever comes in touch with them is sure to never be the same ignorant person.

*Kṛṣṇa-śakti vinā nahe tāra pravartana.* Such is the power of a pure devotee of Lord Kṛṣṇa. This is why following in the footsteps of your great disciples—Harikeśa Swami, Jayapatāka Swami, Hridayānanda Goswami, and many, many other great heroes—we want to dedicate our life to this book distribution mission. We want people to eat unlimited amounts of *prasādam* so that they start understanding your books, and we want to preach the glories of your books everywhere so that people start worshiping them. Your books are the perfect weapons in a perfect army of your disciples and granddisciples. Please let us stay in that army forever.

Your thankful grandchildren in Poznan, Poland.

## Prague

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*tvam apy adabhra-śruta viśrutaṁ vibho*  
*samāpyate yena vidāṁ bubhutsitam*  
*ākhyāhi duḥkhair muhur arditātmanāṁ*  
*saṅkleśa nirvāṇam uśanti nānyathā*

**“Please, therefore, describe the almighty Lord’s activities which you have learned by your vast knowledge of the** ***Vedas,*** **for that will satisfy the hankerings of great learned men and at the same time mitigate the miseries of the masses of common people who are always suffering from material pangs. Indeed, there is not other way to get out of such miseries” (*****Bhāg*****. 1.5.40).**

In the last paragraph of your purport, you write these wonderful words: “Śrī Nāradajī advised Vyāsadeva to describe the glories of the Lord just to do good to all eight classes of men, both good and bad.” The eight classes of men refer to Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s description of the four classes of pious men (*sukṛtinā*) and four classes of impious men (*duṣkṛtinā*). The conclusion for us, your followers, is simple: Your presentations of Śrīla Vyāsadeva’s *Bhāgavatam* are meant for everyone, both good and the bad, the devotees as well as the demons. We do not discriminate: we translate, edit, print, and distribute *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* in every language; we do not care if the people are good or bad. Because we want to be your most humble servants, we want to serve your mission of doing good for others; therefore we try, imperfect as we may be, to “describe the almighty Lord’s activities” as you have described them, in your books. “There is no other way.”

Your humble servants in Prague, the Czech Republic.

## Rādhadeśa Dhāma

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to this day, which marks the anniversary of your divine appearance in this temporary world full of misery and the sense of separateness from Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

“He reasons ill who tells that Vaiṣṇavas die  
When thou art living still in sound.  
The Vaiṣṇava dies to live, and living,  
Tries to spread the holy name around.”

Just as the holy name of Lord Kṛṣṇa is nondifferent from His personal Self, so your divine purports are nondifferent from your personal association. They are the essence of you personally, steeped in unalloyed ecstatic love for Śrī Kṛṣṇa and the mood of unlimited, ever-increasing compassion of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

We desire to follow in the footsteps of those great souls who have strongly imbibed your instructions. They stick to your lotus feet through everything (and that means everything). They tirelessly serve, manage, hear, preach, travel, write, accept disciples, etc., etc. With great humility and self-control, they selflessly tread the path of your sacred footsteps. They desire only to please you because of love for you and the firm conviction that Kṛṣṇa is yours and you can give Him freely. Always catching your mercy, they, with great enthusiasm, make strong endeavors to infuse it throughout the ISKCON Society. They never forget you, and so you enter their purified hearts. Residing there, you continue to direct the transcendental movement you founded out of your exalted devotion and dependence on your mighty Guru Mahārāja.

“In the society of the servitors and served in Kṛṣṇa consciousness, one is not subjected to the pains and pleasures of material society. … Therefore everyone, man or woman, should seek to be an associate in Your society of servitors and served.” (*Kṛṣṇa Book,* Page 184)

May we always cling to the association of devotees of Kṛṣṇa under the protection of the perfect *siddhānta,* ever proclaimed in your Bhaktivedanta purports. May we maintain the simplicity of comprehend that willful separation from such association is brought about due to our affinity for the endless flickering influence of the Lord’s illusory energy, to which we are particularly prone in the present Age of Kali. And as you always desire, may that association be developed and expanded so that living entities everywhere may begin to derive its supreme benefit.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your humble servants in Rādhadeśa Dhāma, Belgium.

## Rosario

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble and respectful obeisances prostrated in the dust of your divine lotus feet.

This is first time we are writing a Vyāsa-pūjā offering for you. In this temple approximately thirty devotees are living, but no one is a disciple of yours. We have taken shelter of the lotus feet of some of your beloved disciples. We feel very unlucky due to our not having had your personal association, but at the same time very fortunate because we are getting to know you through your disciples and books, which speak of your greatness and extraordinary surrender to Lord Kṛṣṇa.

Your relationship with us is like that of a grandfather and his grandsons. You are our favorite grandfather, in whose lap we can sit down and to whom we can tell our little spiritual realizations as we caress his delicate lotus hands. This is not an impertinence; this is how we see you and feel you.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, there are devotees in this city who daily go out and distribute your books, and we can assure you that you are becoming more and more famous. There is no greater happiness for a *saṅkīrtana* devotee than to see somebody in the street take one of your book, look at your picture and say, “Oh, Prabhupāda! Yes, I know this author and already have some of his books.” More than one million books have already been distributed in Argentina, and all over the world the number of your books that have been distributed is astronomical. Your books are real best-sellers. They are bringing about a revolution in modern society, a change imperceptible to mundane eyes. But we devotees are aware of the “havoc” this book distribution will cause in a short time. Out of your causeless mercy thousands of people throughout the world are becoming vegetarian, and thousands look respectfully at India’s real treasure, her spiritual culture.

From the material point of view, what you have done is inconceivable. You got off the boat in New York with just a few dollars and a few books … and soon you had formed a worldwide spiritual movement! But we devotees know you are not a common man. You descended from the spiritual world exclusively to save us; you became our servant—by becoming our spiritual master—despite your exalted position. In the last festival held in Māyāpur, Pṛthu Prabhu narrated a very moving story about you. You were in Stockholm lecturing on the four *varṇas* and the difference between them, when all of a sudden a young man stood up from his seat and said, “I guess you must belong to the first class.” You remained silent for a while and, as a tear slid down your cheek, replied that you belonged neither to the first class, nor to the second, nor to the third, nor even to the fourth. You said you were a fifth-class man, a servant of all other classes, the servant of all mankind.

When a devotee becomes so humble, Kṛṣṇa gives him everything. The Lord feels so indebted to him that He does not know how to reward him. That was your case. Due to your adopting such a position, Kṛṣṇa gave you a movement which is growing bigger and bigger. Some may think your worship to be excessive, but as far as we are concerned, what we can do to glorify you is very little considering the risks you ran just to take us out from the clutches of illusion.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, by the time you passed away there were about one hundred temples all around the world, but today there are over three hundred. This is because many of your disciples kept alive the spirit of preaching you instilled in them. Many years ago you preached to a young man in Moscow, and the result of your powerful preaching is that more than seventy temples have been established throughout the former Soviet Union. A Vaiṣṇava is very powerful, and you are the best among the Vaiṣṇavas. You and your books are bringing about a revolution in this impious society. Therefore, we ask that you bless us so that we may continue to perform devotional service in this *saṅkīrtana* mission. In this way we will be able to offer some little service unto the lotus feet of our dear grandfather, who did not stop preaching even for one second so that today thousands and thousands of his grandchildren can take advantage of the bliss of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Hoping that you may forgive the offenses committed to the divine dust of your lotus feet, we remain  
Your servants in Rosario, Argentina.

## Saltillo

*etāvaty eva śuśrūṣā kāryā pitari putrakaiḥ*  
*bāḍham ity anumanyeta gauraveṇa guror vacaḥ*

**“Sons ought to render service to their father exactly to this extent. One should obey the command of his father or spiritual master with due deference, saying, “Yes sir” (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **3.24.13).**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humbly obeisances. All glories to you. Now we can observe how you have the Absolute Truth. Lord Kṛṣṇa is a reality, the Supreme Godhead. First of all, Śrī Kṛṣṇa is a historical fact. Historical places like Mathurā, Vṛndavāna, Dvārakā, etc. exist, and they are sources of archeological finds giving evidence of Kṛṣṇa’s *līlā*. Some discoveries of science confirm the statements of Kṛṣṇa’s teachings in *Bhagavad-gītā* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*. So Kṛṣṇa conscious knowledge is perfect, without mistakes, illusions, sensory imperfection, or cheating. Therefore we are so fortunate to be your servants. Your order to us was to remain together inside in your sacred Vaiṣṇava-saṅga (ISKCON). You spoke out about the danger of trying to be independent of this movement, how it leads one to become *asāra,* useless, and to live a great hallucination (*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 4.9.11, purport). Objectively everybody can see the end of deviation from your movement: suicide, murder, TV watching, sleep, *sahajiyā*-ism, premature adoption of *gopī-bhāva,* etc. All such deviants lost. So we are begging you, please let us stay forever as your faithful servants and become fully absorbed in carrying out your orders—preaching, distributing books, going on *harināma,* putting on college programs, TV preaching, etc. In this way, under your wings in ISKCON, let us help you expand Lord Caitanya’s movement and be the servants of your servants.

Thank you very much, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Your servants in Saltillo, Mexico.

## Scotland

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful obeisances. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and to your ever-continuous mercy which is coming through your books. All glories to your ISKCON society and to all the Vaiṣṇavas within this big house.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you made your auspicious appearance in the year 1896. In that year Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura also reached out to the Western world by sending to McGill University his book entitled *Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu: His Life and Precepts*. This was some spiritual “coincidence”—having the two events in the same year—and the spiritual significance of these two events is noted in the prediction of Śrīla Sac-cid-ānanda Ṭhākura Bhaktivinoda. He said that Lord Caitanya did not advent Himself to liberate only a few men in India. Rather, His main objective was to emancipate all living entities of all countries throughout the entire universe and to preach the **eternal religion**. Lord Caitanya said that in every town and village His name will be sung.

Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura hoped for the day to come when the fortunate English, Russian, German, and American people would take up banners, *mṛdaṅgas* and *karatālas* and raise *kīrtana* through the streets and towns. He anticipated that the day would come when the fair-skinned men from across the ocean would raise up the chanting of *Jaya Śacīnandana! Jaya Śacīnandana kī jaya!* and join the Bengali devotees. He wondered when the day would come when he could witness the holy transcendental ecstasy of the Vaiṣṇava *dharma* being the only *dharma* and all other sects and religions flowing like rivers into the ocean of Vaiṣṇava *dharma*.

Śrīla Bhaktivinoda also predicted; “A personality will soon appear to preach the teachings of Lord Caitanya and move unrestrictedly over the whole world with His message.”

It can be seen that the personality who actually moved “unrestrictedly over the whole world” is undoubtedly you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Your Guru Mahārāja, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, upon first meeting you, instructed you to go preach in the Western world. Later, before leaving this world, he further emphasized and ordered that if you ever get money you should print books, because the press is the *bṛhad-mṛdaṅga* and can be heard all over the world. It is louder than the *kīrtana* of a few men.

Taking this order of your Guru Mahārāja as your life and soul, you came to save us fallen, conditioned souls all over this world.

On a steamer in the middle of the Atlantic Ocean, on your way to America, you wrote a prayer. In this prayer one verse states, “By his \[Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura’s\] strong desire, the holy name of Lord Gaurāṅga will spread throughout all the countries of the Western world. In all the cities, towns, and villages on the earth, from all the oceans, seas, rivers, and streams; everyone will chant the holy name of Kṛṣṇa.” When you arrived in Boston you composed another prayer for the deliverance of all souls. In this prayer you said, “How will they understand the mellows of devotional service? O Lord, I am simply praying for your mercy so that I will be able to convince them about your message. I wish that you may deliver them. Therefore if you so desire their deliverance, then only will they be able to understand your message.”

Kṛṣṇa has listened to your prayer, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and made your mission here to the West successful, because you have so much compassion upon us fallen souls.

When a pure devotee desires another’s welfare or prays, Kṛṣṇa always reciprocates by answering His devotee’s prayers. Your Guru Mahārāja’s desire was strong, and therefore you came, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to save us. Your desire was strong, and therefore this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is taking root in every country of the world.

In one conversation Viṣṇujana Swami remarked that Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura said he was leaving this world with his work unfinished. Then you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, replied, “Then let us finish it. We are descendants of Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura. So he kept it unfinished so that we shall get the chance to finish it. That is his mercy. He could have finished it immediately. He is Vaiṣṇava, he is all-powerful.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you traveled this world fourteen times, and you planted the seed of Kṛṣṇa consciousness in every continent. By your mercy there are temples in every major city, and by your emphasis on book distribution all the fallen souls are receiving the opportunity to associate with you and be saved by you when they read your books.

You are an all-powerful Vaiṣṇava who could make this world Kṛṣṇa conscious immediately. However, your mercy has been saved for us also—so that we may take your life force, your books, and deliver them to everyone. Thus the predictions have come true. By your books many devotees are made, they chant the names of Kṛṣṇa, and the real United Nations gathers in Māyāpur to glorify Śrī Śacīnandana Gaurāṅga Mahāprabhu. You have popularized the chanting and fulfilled the desires of Śrīla Bhaktivinoda to allow the day to come when fair-skinned men will join the Bengali devotees in chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa and singing *jaya śacīnandana*. Your mercy has allowed English, French, German, and American devotees—and many more—to raise *kīrtana* and take up banners, *mṛdaṅgas,* and *karatālas*. You fulfilled your Guru Mahārāja’s desire by printing so many books in so many languages to be distributed for the good fortune of all fallen souls, and still your mercy is flowing continuously.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for such great kindness upon us. Thank you for allowing your mercy to flood us here in Scotland, too. By your mercy devotees are distributing more books than ever; book distribution is a success here. More devotees are joining to help this book distribution of yours. By your mercy we have nice preaching programs which center on book distribution. And by your mercy everything is coming out successfully by book distribution, as you said.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

We pray for the strength to help fulfill your desire to get your books distributed profusely and to be able to always bring you pleasure by increasing the distribution of your books.

Your insignificant servants in Scotland.  
(written by Pālindrī-devī dāsī)

## Secunderabad

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances on the occasion of this most auspicious Vyāsa-pūjā day of Your Divine Grace. Whatever our position may be, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we pray unto you to always enable us to remember that our goal is to please you only and that this is our only qualification to approach Kṛṣṇa.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have built “a house in which the whole world can live peacefully,” so please give us the realization that to bring more and more sincere spirit souls under the shelter of this “eternal family”—the International Society for Krishna Consciousness—is the only way of perfecting our largely wasted and uselessly squandered lives.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, your instructions are simply so soothing, just like a magic pain balm that completely relieves the excruciating pain caused by a chronic, hopeless affliction, which in our case is the disgusting agitation resulting from the “heart disease” of sense gratification, which we have been suffering since time immemorial.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, out of your boundless and causeless compassion you have raised us (all the unfortunate species of Kali-yuga) from the lowest state of existence to the highest platform of Kṛṣṇa *bhakti,* which is unattainable even for the demigods. How are we to express our gratitude? And now that all of us (all ignorant fools) have this greatest benediction, our foul rascal mind thinks of misusing this high position to gratify our false ego by lording it over that which is all yours. Our praying to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is the only shield and shelter to protect us from this rascal mind’s (Māyā’s) destructive dictation.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you delivered to us the medicine of chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa, and we (all completely bewildered living entities) do not yet realize how much we have to be attached to your lotus feet or how much we are indebted to Your Divine Grace.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, O destroyer of the ghostlike witch called “material desire,” you are breaking down the overwhelmingly persistent barrier that has confined our loving propensity to dull and lifeless matter. The invincible and matchless instructions emanating from your lotus mouth flow towards the supreme destination, the Supreme Person, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we don’t think we can express our gratitude merely by words but only by cooperating to keep ISKCON (your matchless gift) united and especially by distributing your books.

Your insignificant servants in Secunderabad, India.  
(written by Gopinātha dāsa)

## Singapore

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories unto you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Hare Kṛṣṇa.

On this most auspicious day we are humbly begging for your kind mercy on all of us. We have now purchased this nice place in Singapore and have invited Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Madanamohana to preside here. The place is in a very posh area, with many Indians living nearby.

The temple is an old bungalow house with 16,000 sq. ft. of floor space and a basement. We have about nine rooms, and there is a nice garden. There are two very nice mango trees, one coconut tree, a lime tree, and many plantain trees as well. We have also some *campaka* trees. The garden has a nice lawn of carpet grass, and it is very pleasant to view. The beach is only a fifteen-minute walk.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, kindly come and spend time here with us. We would especially like to offer you some of the mangoes; they are all very nice.

Begging for your divine association, we remain  
Your children in Singapore.

## Stockholm

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

We are eternally indebted to you for giving us the wonderful shelter of engagement in devotional service. How awakening it is to go out and distribute your books! This process of book distribution is most enlivening, since it forces us to surrender to the lotus feet of the Lord, freeing us from the gross and subtle coverings imposed upon us by the modes of material nature. In the heart of a *saṅkīrtana* devotee there is ultimately no possibility of maintaining the false designation of his identity. Sooner than he might think he is breaking through the covering of his false ego, and he finds himself with tears in his eyes, standing on the street, realizing his eternal position as your servant.
