These tears may be not due to transcendental ecstasy but rather mental and physical pain, but they wash away the last remnants of his illusion, his false hope to become one with God and master of the creation. Assuming his original relationship with Guru and Kṛṣṇa, the *saṅkīrtana* devotee gives up his last shelters in this world comes under the control of the Supersoul, reaching out with a merciful attitude for the conditioned souls, giving them, after their long deathly march through the desert of impersonalism, the nectarlike taste of freedom and peace emanating from your books.

Still followed and attacked by material desires, the *saṅkīrtana* devotee cries out for your mercy—and see! As book after book he distributes, day after day he spends on *saṅkīrtana,* his heart becomes lighter and lighter and the pain of unfulfilled desires diminishes.

The distribution of your books is so wonderful. It can open the eyes of even the most dull-headed person. Finally one starts to see the ghastly face of Māyā and the pitiful condition of society today. Simply by distributing your books and reading them daily, the *saṅkīrtana* devotee becomes the perfect seer of the conditioned souls’ problems. Disregarding the hypocritical talk of Kali-yuga’s so-called intellectuals, so-called scientists’ and so-called leaders, he is the true intellectual accepting the vision of Śrīla Vyāsadeva, which is given to us in your books. And so a purified *saṅkīrtana* devotee is automatically protected from the pseudo-spiritual preaching of śūdralike men in the garb of transcendentalists. Even the hysterical religionists do not appeal to him, since he knows Kṛṣṇa to reside in everybody’s heart, above pseudo religious disputes. What does *karma, jñāna,* and *yoga* mean for a dedicated book distributor? He realizes how dangerous his mind is, how wonderful and sublime the holy name is, saving us from the mental pits.

How protected one is while distributing your books! And even if after crossing the initial stages of book distribution one finds oneself gliding into some sort of routine, which is nothing but another manifestation of impersonalism—even then, by simply engaging in this wonderful mission, a *saṅkīrtana* devotee will be brought again back to his senses by being tossed here and there in the ocean of conditioned souls, helplessly raising his hands, reaching out with your books, until the next person takes one and the stream of mercy starts to flow again.

Simply by distributing your books, Śrīla Prabhupāda, true humility manifests in the heart of your dedicated follower and the artificial bodily concepts of surrender get washed away. And after days and years of struggle, your *saṅkīrtana* devotee will be rewarded with the most wonderful gift—the realization of Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s supreme personality, which gives complete freedom from all the poisonous ideas of voidism, impersonalism, and atheism. Then, ecstatic at heart, such a *saṅkīrtana* “fanatic” simply keeps on distributing your books, full of gratitude to you for this most wonderful gift—the distribution of your transcendental books.

And what does the book distribution do for the Kali-yuga man?

Even if he unknowingly touches or acquires these books, his whole life becomes very auspicious. And due to the fact that he somehow got in touch with your books, his readiness for Kṛṣṇa consciousness increases, his sinful reactions diminish, and true knowledge is established in his heart. Even though he may not fully understand, the moment he took that book, being drowned by the mercy of that *saṅkīrtana* devotee, he experienced for first time after a long, long time the taste of devotional service. He will never forget—his spiritual life has started. And what if he redistributes the book he got? Then he unknowingly engages in the *saṅkīrtana* mission and receives all its benefits.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, the amount of blessings somebody may receive while engaged in this book distribution is hard to perceive. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you for these books, and thank you for the wonderful life of a *saṅkīrtana* devotee you gave to us. Distributing book after book, we may try to repay this eternal debt to you—but how many books are there being produced? As many as we distribute, that many will come. And so the debt never diminishes. And so, being Your ecstatic debtors, the *saṅkīrtana* devotees will forever travel across the whole planet. Though debtors, they appear to be rather blissful and satisfied, since they are in their constitutional position as the eternal distributors of the mercy Your Divine Grace bestowed upon us.

All glories to your books!  
All glories to the book distribution mission!  
All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your servants in Stockholm, Sweden.  
(written by Maṇidhara dāsa)

## Uppsala

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

While writing this year’s Vyāsa-pūjā offering to you I am remembering the two letters I received from you, by your mercy, in the years 1976 and 1977. At that time I was going to Eastern Europe to preach, together with devotees from Germany. You very much liked that program and encouraged us to go on preaching.

“People are suffering greatly presently due to having lost all proper vision,” you wrote. “They are completely without knowledge as to what is the real purpose of life. They have forgotten what is the real purpose of life! It is up to you reestablish their lost relationship with Kṛṣṇa by being sincere to preach the message of Kṛṣṇa purely, by yourself following the instructions of the Lord and his bona-fide representative, the spiritual master. If you follow nicely our Kṛṣṇa conscious program and preach to these unfortunate people, surely Kṛṣṇa will recognize your service.”

So you gave us hope, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that just by following your example and always preaching in a humble way, Kṛṣṇa will surely help us.

“A little attempt is required.” you wrote. “Then all other things will come from Kṛṣṇa. This is my practical experience. I made a little attempt and Kṛṣṇa has sent me so many representatives to help me.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your, as you put it, “little attempt” has saved, is saving, and will save many, many suffering people for thousands of years.

I just pray that I may always remember your humble instructions in these letters and just be a menial servant of yours and continue spreading the glories of you and your ISKCON movement to my best capacity.

*Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your servants in Uppsala, Sweden.  
(written by Prapūjaka dāsa)

## Vilnius

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful obeisances at your divine lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace on this most auspicious Vyāsa-pūjā day, the anniversary of your appearance.

Your glories are unlimited, and your mercy has spread all around the planet. Your greatness; submissiveness to your *guru;* modesty; unselfishness, determination, and enthusiasm in executing your purpose; softheartedness, compassion, liberality, mercifulness, and above all the superhuman nature of your personality attracts everyone to spiritual life, opening the royal path to Lord Kṛṣṇa. That we see practically happening.

Your mission is to fight for religious principles, smashing the existential basis of society of cats and dogs who live only for their own self-satisfaction. The voluminous literatures you have kindly presented dismantle the demoniac fortresses, neutralizing the powerful armies of Māyā (sex, wealth, and atheism) and bring about a revolution in the hearts of human beings, leading them home, back to Godhead. After reading your books people surrender to Kṛṣṇa. Here are some typical statements from readers: “I will surrender to Kṛṣṇa all my remaining life. This is not a high-sounding empty statement but the truth coming from the bottom of my heart.” “After reading *Bhagavad-gita* As It Is I understood that Kṛṣṇa consciousness is exactly what I need and that I should serve Kṛṣṇa as much as I can.”

Lord Kṛṣṇa has appeared in the form of the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the herald of this revolution. You have set a standard of behavior for us: to regularly and attentively chant Hare Kṛṣṇa, to study the holy scriptures you have delivered, to preach the glories of Lord Kṛṣṇa and work hard for Him, to go out on *harināma*, to produce and distribute books and *prasādam*—and then everything will be all right.

Please kindly engage us in this mission; give us your blessings to be involved in it more and more.

Your devotees in Vilnius, Lithuania.

## Zurich

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Twenty-two years ago the first devotees came to Switzerland (from Paris) to open a branch of your ISKCON. What happened in this country since then is truly a miracle. The Swiss are known for working hard to accumulate wealth; religion doesn’t even come second. But they like your transcendental books! By the sincere effort of the devotees to please you, there is one of your wonderful books in practically every house. Who would have thought in the beginning that your plans to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness would come true so fast. We have full faith in your words: “Just distribute books and everything else will come.” And, indeed, our other activities—*harināma,* preaching programs, and *prasādam* distribution—are going on successfully too. Since about a year ago, more than a dozen young people have joined your Hare Kṛṣṇa movement in Zurich. With these devotees and many more to come, within the next twenty-two years we can certainly fulfill our *saṅkīrtana* goal of putting all of your transcendental books in every household in Switzerland.

Although we’d like to offer this service to you, we are fully aware that without your help we can never do it. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, give us your blessings and the auspicious strength to put full sets of your books in every home in the coming years.

Your humble servants in Zurich, Switzerland.  
(written by Śaṅkarāraṇya dāsa)

## Vyāsa-Pūjā Homages from the Publishers

## Bhaktivedanta Book Trust, Bombay

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Śrīla Narottama Dāsa Ṭhākura has sung:

*sat-saṅga chādi kainu asate vilāsate*  
*karane lagila ye karma-bandha-phāṅsa*

**“I gave up the association of devotees and tried to enjoy material life. As a result, I became entangled in the meshes of fruitive activities and their reactions.”**

Being entangled in fruitive activities, we have accepted one body after another. We were born in nice families from a material point of view. Sufficient wealth was there—nice father, mother, brother, sisters. Yet with all this we weren’t happy.

So it was seen that material advantages alone could not make us happy. Such activities simply cause entanglement and transmigration from one body to another. This frustration in material activities is actually mercy on us, because it is the cause of our inquiry about the real value of life—*brahma jijñāsā*. In the *Vedas* (*Mundaka Upaniṣad* 11.2.12), *tad vijñānārthaṁ sa gurum evābhigacchet: “*To learn the transcendental subject matter one must approach a spiritual master.” We have found the best spiritual master—your good self, Śrīla Prabhupāda—for you have given us all the knowledge by which we can get out of the entanglement of fruitive activities. We pray to you on this holiest day of your appearance to help us understand the real business of human life. As stated in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (1.2.10), *jīvasya tattva-jijñāsā nārtho yaś ceha karmabhiḥ.* Our only business should be in the human form of life is to inquire from the bona fide spiritual master about extrication from the entanglement of *karma-bandha-phāṅsa,* fruitive activities. Without this inquiry in our lives, we become nothing more then a two-legged animal. Dogs, hogs, and tigers are always busy looking for something to eat, a place to sleep, and some sex life. In this human form, however, we have gotten the chance to use our intelligence to ask, Who am I? Why have I come to this world? What is my duty? Who is the supreme controller? and What is the difference between matte rand spirit? In *Bhagavad-gītā* (15.15) it is stated, *sarvasya cāhaṁ hṛdi-sanniviṣṭo mattaḥ smṛtir jñānam apohanaṁ ca:* “I am seated in everyone’s heart, and from Me comes knowledge, remembrance, and forgetfulness.” In the animal form the living being completely forgets his relationship with God. This is called *apohanam,* or forgetfulness. As human beings we have developed consciousness and so have a chance to understand God and our relationship with Him. By your mercy we may become inquisitive about self-realization and avoid becoming a two-legged animal. The instructions of the spiritual master are nondifferent from the instructions of the Supreme Lord. So just as Arjuna, who was in great difficulty after the disappearance of the Lord, got relief by remembering the instructions he received on the Battlefield of Kurukṣetra. Similarly, simply by remembering your instructions we can get relief from the anxieties created by desires for fruitive activities.

Your servants at the Bhaktivedanta Book Trust, Bombay, India.

## Bhaktivedanta Book Trust, North America

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Life is short, we make it very hard.  
It’s simple for the pure in heart.  
There’s a purpose, a reason that we’re here,  
More than just playing a part.

The mission at hand has been spoken;  
That every woman ‘n man in all lands get a book.  
The mission of man has been woven,  
In every village and town all around the world.

There’s a conquest of cultural concern,  
A renaissance of humankind—  
East and West in chanting and your books.  
The world will note the change in time.

The mission at hand has been spoken;  
That every woman ‘n man in all lands get a book.  
The mission of man has been woven,  
In every village and town all around the world.

I can’t serve my senses anymore.  
It’s only chewing the chewed.  
My life is meant for more than this,  
Fulfilling the mission of Kṛṣṇa and you.

The mission at hand has been spoken;  
That every woman ‘n man in all lands get a book.  
The mission of man has been woven,  
In every village and town all around the world.

There’s a place beyond this mundane world,  
Free from all lust, anger, and greed.  
One engaged in your mission, life and soul,  
Will reach that place with certain guarantee.

The mission at hand has been spoken;  
That every woman ‘n man in all lands get a book.  
The mission of man has been woven,  
In every village and town all around the world.

Your servants at the Bhaktivedanta Book Trust, Los Angeles, California.

## Bhaktivedanta Book Trust, North Europe

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

On your *vyāsāsana* in the temple room your *karatālas* always sit, ready for you to take them up and begin *kīrtana* at any moment. But on some occasions the *karatālas* are pushed aside or even disappear from the *āsana*. This happens when we pile up newly published books to offer to you on behalf of Śrīla Harikeśa Swami, our BBT trustee. Then there is no room left for the *karatālas*.

But what is the harm? Publishing books is big *kīrtana*. Through them your voice reaches the most distant corners of this world, through them you preach in the most magnificent way. Most of us here have never met you, and we had no idea what Kṛṣṇa consciousness was when you left the planet. But later your devotees found us and gave us the most precious gift: your association, your books. Having been swept up by the power of your mercy into the embrace of your mission, we try to publish your books for distribution in as many languages as possible. And just see, Vaiṣṇavas are manifesting even in the most remote parts of this planet, seemingly popping up out of nowhere, making your books the basis of their lives. And they in turn dedicatethemselves to the production and distribution of your wonderful literature. Who would have thought several years ago that so many new *yatras* would appear where your books are so desperately wanted! What is the use of so many countries in place of the former USSR if the people there cannot read your books in their native languages? What is the use of these languages altogether if they do not convey your transcendental message? Who knows, maybe all these countries will disappear from the face of the earth unless we translate and send your books there very soon?

Your books transform the lives of people all over the world. The tree of Lord Caitanya, planted by you in so many countries, is growing in a mystical way—like the mango tree He planted in the courtyard of Śrīvāsa Ṭhākura. Its seeds are your books. They are the ripened mango fruit of all Vedic literatures, handed down in disciplic succession, and the very means of conquest.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bless our faulty efforts. May we never let you stop speaking.

\* \* \*

Here is one of the many letters we receive daily. We thought maybe you would like to hear it:

Dear publishers,

I was told that you stopped printing the books that give the people an understanding of another, invisible world, an understanding we need so much in this world of relativity.

I am living in a village. My elder brother has kind of mental disease. I have also a mother, younger brother (10 years old) and a younger sister (3.5 years old). My mother is ill, and she is working like anything to feed her children. I am working as a locksmith in the fur workshop, and I don’t have any possibility to buy your books or to have a teacher or master in this science. I can hardly leave my village.

I am writing to you so that you can understand that there are a lot of people like me in this country. I am not begging for alms. No, other people also don’t need any financial support. That is nonsense, a waste of money, which doesn’t bring profit to mankind. Why are people asking for financial assistance? Because they are spiritually poor. They don’t have any spiritual support.

Actually, nobody needs books on the culture and art of India. Who needs their culture? What’s that for? But the books you have been printing instruct the people about a bona fide path, better than it is done by other religions.

Recently I tried to order these books on Vedic science, and I came to know that they are not in print anymore. I cannot borrow books like *Bhagavad-gītā* and *The Science of Self-Realization* and copy them. I don’t have enough time and strength for that. I don’t have a phenomenal memory either, so I can’t learn them by heart.

Dear publishers, I am appealing to you. The spiritual condition of our country is very low, so I beg you: Please don’t stop printing such books, because they give light to the people, and most people will agree with that.

Boris, 17 years, Byelorussia

Dear Srila Prabhupada and dear Boris, we have no plans to stop the printing. We shall go on printing until every human being has a full set—and a spare set.

Your humble servants in the Bhaktivedanta Book Trust, North Europe

## Vyāsa-Pūjā Homages from Other Sources

## Back to Godhead Magazine

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to your divine appearance day, which brought a brilliant beam of transcendental light to break through the Kali-yuga darkness.

You came to us at a time when, though proud of our wealth and technology, we had little to hope for. Misguided by godless leaders, we could not foresee the unimaginable suffering awaiting us. Nor had we any knowledge of the spiritual world and the limitless happiness awaiting those who qualify to enter it.

Though we were totally undeserving, you came to tell us of Kṛṣṇa’s world. And to persuade us to go there, you created *Back to Godhead* magazine.

I have got the clue of going ‘Back to Godhead’ just after leaving my present material body, and in order to take along with me all my contemporary men and women of the world, I have started my paper, ‘Back to Godhead,’ as one of the means to the way.

Now, by your mercy, the message of Godhead has reached us, and we pray for the faith you had—that *Back to Godhead* can deliver all contemporary men and women.

We know they can be delivered only if we present the message of Kṛṣṇa purely, as you have given it to us. You wanted us to be uncompromising in teaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness through your *Back to Godhead*. And you wanted *Back to Godhead* to become as popular as the most successful American magazines. So we naturally wonder, How will millions of people ever want to subscribe to a magazine that smashes their cherished misconceptions? The answer must be that the whole Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement—not just *Back to Godhead*—will be successful in enlightening millions of people and attracting them to the pure message of *Back to Godhead*. When people want Kṛṣṇa—when they’re ready for the truth—they’ll want *Back to Godhead*.

We pray that we may cooperate as you asked and that your movement may quickly fulfill its destiny. Then people will be filled with hope, and we’ll see the world moving in the only desirable direction: back home, back to Godhead.

We beg to remain,  
Your aspiring servants at *Back to Godhead* magazine

## Bhaktivedanta Archives

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

We feel especially unworthy that somehow of all your many disciples and followers, we have been given the mercy to take care of the many personal papers, manuscripts, letters, photos, slides, tape recordings, and other paraphernalia of Your Divine Grace. This is the first time in history that such a treasure house of personal and philosophical instruction has been left by a pure devotee of Kṛṣṇa for future generations. For many of us, members of ISKCON during your “whirlwind visit” to this planet, it seems almost that because these archival materials are available in the various media, we have the facility to have more of your association now than when you were personally present. By Kṛṣṇa’s mercy in the form of modern technologies, you are living with us practically in both *vāṇī* and *vapu* forms.

It is our fervent prayer that we remain attached to this service to you and your followers. The appreciation of your followers has been especially encouraging over the years. When your followers have been pleased, it is the indication to us that you are pleased. Many of them are working so very hard in book distribution and direct preaching efforts to make the world Kṛṣṇa conscious; please give us your mercy so that we can help them in some way in their wonderful endeavors.

We are especially moved to see the spread of Kṛṣṇa consciousness in areas like the old Communist bloc in the last few years. You and your inspiration are indeed the fulfillment of the prophecies of Lord Caitanya and Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura. Let us always remember to hold fast to your lotus feet.

Your humble servants at the Bhaktivedanta Archives.

## Bhaktivedanta Institute (Bombay and Oakland)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

All glories to Your Divine Grace! All glories to the most auspicious appearance day of Your Divine Grace!

Your glories are unlimited and no one can chant all of your pastimes. Some devotees have the feeling that the *Līlāmṛta,* the biography of Your Divine Grace, is incomplete, but that is not surprising. It is like Ananta Śeṣa’s many mouths trying to recount the glories of Lord Kṛṣṇa. By your causeless mercy we have been brought under the shelter of Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* movement. We had been brain-washed by modern science to think that life is chemical machinery and that enjoyment of this body is the pupose of life. But Your Divine Grace has taught us that the Absolute Truth is attained by hearing from realized souls, that life comes from life, and that the purpose of life is to serve Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Life, the Supreme Absolute Truth, the Supreme Personality of Godhead. We are most unqualified to push on this Bhaktivedanta Institute, but Your Divine Grace has engaged us in this preaching arm of ISKCON.

No matter how intelligent we may be, it is not possible for us to defeat the onslaught of atheistic science, just as it was beyond Arjuna’s power to defeat the fiercely powerful enemies at Kurukṣetra. But, as Mahārāja Parīkṣit described, it was because of Kṛṣṇa that Arjuna was able to come out victorious. So in the same way, we can expect victory only by the mercy of Your Divine Grace.

We are praying that you may become pleased with us so that our attempts at hosting scientists, holding conferences, giving campus lectures, and producing scientific literature on the nature and origin of life to defeat the false propaganda of modern science will all become fruitful. In this way the intelligent and the young may become attracted to reading your books, the only hope for this fallen age. You have built a house in which the whole world can live. The Bhaktivedanta Institute is a door to that house for those reluctant to take the main entrance.

On this most auspicious day of your divine appearance, we are praying that when we feel desperate in our struggles in Kṛṣṇa consciousness we may be blessed with your compassionate glance. Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bless us so that we may continue to render some menial service by pushing on this Bhaktivedanta Institute, the most important preaching arm of your ISKCON.

Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*!

Your servants at the Bhaktivedanta Institute, Bombay and Oakland, California.

## Bhaktivedanta Institute (San Diego)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Fool that I am, I have grabbed onto one of your difficult orders: “Challenge the rascal scientists!” If I had some good sense, I would have secretly taken this order into my heart and quietly hoped I would someday, somehow be able to do something about it. But no. I had to stand in front of an assembly of your leading devotees and make some bold declarations.

And now I feel like I have jumped out of an airplane with no parachute.

With no ripcord to pull, I can only hang on to your order for dear life and hope that by some miracle of Your Divine Grace I will not perish.

My only solace is that I am not alone, and that others here at the San Diego BI are also falling through the wild blue yonder of surrender to your merciful, pristine, soul-saving order.

Your servants at the Bhaktivedanta Institute, San Diego, California.  
(written by Drutakarmā dāsa)

## Efland Gurukula

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. You deserve all worship, all respect, and all obedience. All the holy places reside in you. Only by pleasing you can we gain the pleasure of Lord Caitanya, of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa. Without your pleasure, we are lost, on a boat without a captain, with no knowledge of our destination.

What can we offer such a Vaiṣṇava, a Vaikuṇṭha man, as yourself? Repayment, adequate worship, is impossible. But, we make the attempt out of duty, out of love.

We offer you ourselves. Take our life, body, words, and intelligence. Take our creativity and academic knowledge.

In our *gurukula* we beg that you accept our every action. We also beg that you accept the fruits, students who are blissfully enthusiastic about attaining pure devotional service in this life and who are educated so that they have the tools to become leaders of society.

Please bless us so that our students’ words, character, and behavior stand as living proofs of the efficacy of the science of devotion you teach us. May these students become living examples of your purports. Please let our students show by their personal example that one who rises early, attends Maṅgala Ārati, chants *japa,* hears *Bhāgavatam,* and attempts to use every moment in Kṛṣṇa’s service will achieve pure devotion in this very life.

All glories to your appearance in this world!

The students and teachers at the gurukula in Efland, North Carolina.  
(written by Urmilā-devī dāsī)

## Food For Life (Commonwealth of Independent States)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my most respectful obeisances. All glories to your Food for Life mission.

Right now I’m in Moscow after having visited many of your major centers in the Confederation of Independent States (CIS, the former Soviet Union), including Sukhumi, Georgia, where devotees are risking their lives to serve you. They are caught in the middle of an insurrection and are working under extremely austere conditions, with no gas, electricity, or water. And yet like the true valiant soldiers they are, they are managing to cook *bhoga,* offer it to Kṛṣṇa, and serve *prasādam* to 1,200 stranded victims of war every day. It’s an inspiration to see.

And so I have been envisioning a major revolution that will soon erupt, beginning from this small war-torn city and spreading far and wide, changing the face of the “former Soviet” and indeed the world. In fact this revolution will spell the beginning of the end of materialistic civilization. Let us just imagine for a moment, Śrīla Prabhupāda, the consequences…

Read all about it, read all about it …  
“The FFL revolution!!! Absolutely no one is escaping. Not even the hardest hearts can avoid being captured by the Vaiṣṇava mercy-naries. They are totally possessed in their attitude of non-discrimination. Everyone in the city is being held captive. They are like madmen and -women. They don’t speak a word except ‘*Mahā-prasādam kī jaya!*’ ‘ISKCON bullets *kī jaya*!’ ‘*Hari bol! Hari bol!*’ ‘*Yesho reece, yesho halarva,*’ ‘Secret weapon *kī jaya*!’ It’s just wild!  
“The military have fled. The Vaiṣṇavas have taken complete control of the city. All the meat and liquor shops have been burnt to the ground. The main water supply of the city has been sabotaged with a rose-scented water that has an irresistible intoxicating effect on anyone who drinks even a drop. The streets are filled with men, women, and children rolling on the ground in fits of laughter. They can’t speak anymore. Their throats are filled with a mysterious white liquid substance called “Vāruṇī juice.” And just in case someone tries to avoid their street raids, they strategically place M-bombs (*maṅgala āratī* sweets) in all the commercial office blocks and shops, as well as H-bombs (*hlep,* russian bread) in all the letter boxes. No one is being spared.  
“Apparently these Vaiṣṇava mercy-naries have declared war on all Māyic influences in the CIS. And according to an eyewitness, ‘They’re not going to stop until every man, women, child, old person, poor person, invalid, student, and politician gives up his or her independence and surrenders to chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa!’  
“A military expert in Sukhumi, where the fighting is the most intense, said today, ‘A small band of commandos called “the saffron berets” have succeeded in bewildering the military heads of the Georgian army, including their general. The army is now supplying all the ammunition (rice, flour, semolina, and oil) and heavy artillery (wood-fired stoves and portable bakers’ ovens) necessary for their full-scale attack throughout the country. I believe they will conquer Georgia first and then the entire CIS. Next they’ll move into Eastern Europe, then India, then Australasia, then the United States and Africa, and then finally the rest of the world, inundating every town and village with what they call Kṛṣṇa *prema*. Then when the masses are totally bewildered by Kṛṣṇa *prema,* a Kṛṣṇa government will be organized from the United States that will eventually rule the world.’  
“Sam Ossa reporting for the *Moscow Times,* 1 May 1993.”

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, I must admit that I have also been captured by your secret weapon, known as *prasādam*. And we feel very confident that your Food for Life program will play a major role in spreading the *saṅkīrtana* movement. After all, you yourself said in a letter to Satyahit dated 16 March 1974, “If the people do not even have sufficient food, they will not even be able to receive spiritual instructions. So I’m hopeful that if we can widely distribute free foodstuffs to the people of India, by giving it out at our centers as well as travelling parties to villages, we will win over the whole country and the whole world by this activity on Krsna’s behalf.” And there are so many other references throughout your books and letters to support full-scale *prasādam* distribution.

However, the fact is that an army without a general is not an army at all. Therefore the Food for Life “saffron berets” are awaiting your command and intelligent direction. They’re fully confident of the power of their weaponry and are ready, willing, and able, but your mercy is all they are made of. How can they distribute *prasādam* without first receiving your *prasādam*?

I hope you have enjoyed our humorious vision. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us all Kṛṣṇa’s *prasādam*.

Eternally indebted and with serving spoons in hand, we fall at your lotus feet.

Your prasādam bhaktas in the CIS, and around the world.  
(written by Priyavrata dāsa)

## International Society for Cow Protection

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhsya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

You brought knowledge of a way of life dating back to the ancient Vedic culture of India and thus gave us the most potent and avant-garde vegetarian, animal rights, and environmental movement available to the modern world.

Your Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is capable of presenting an absolute solution, unlike other movements that only decry the mistreatment of animals and the planet and at best fight for the cessation of such treatment.

It can be agreed that all living entities have rights, but how do we all co-exist in this material world of birth, death, old age, and disease? Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the only one possessing the solution to this question.

Your departing desire was to present this absolute solution to the world by establishing the *varṇāśrama* system with Kṛṣṇa as the center, and agriculture and cow protection as the economic base. This absolute solution is one that no other movement has to offer to the world, be it vegetarian, animal rights, humanitarian, religious, or environmental.

“Our Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is not a sentimental movement. It takes care of all-around social organization. It is not a religious sentiment. Everything should be taken care of. Therefore we say there should be cow protection” (from a class on *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* \[1.10.4\], recorded November 25, 1974).

You knew that your books could philosophically convince people of the validity of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and you also knew that the devotee community living a lifestyle structured by the *varṇāśrama* system, which is based on Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and economically provided for by agriculture and cow protection would be capable of convincing people to change their way of life and that of the whole world.

“If we can keep cows sufficiently and grow our necessary foodstuffs, then we shall show a new way of life to your countrymen—completely spiritual life in healthy atmosphere in divine consciousness” (from a letter to Satyabhāmā-devī dāsī, March 30, 1969).

“We can have a great many communities all over the world so that people everywhere can see how, by leading a pure and simple life of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, all one’s needs in life can be satisfied” (from a letter to Paul Reed, January 2, 1972).

Śrīla Prabhupāda, during your last days on earth you stated you had yet to fulfill your mission, you had yet to establish *varṇāśrama*. Let your mission be fulfilled in the efforts of your devotees, and please enable our humble selves to contribute to this fulfillment, and your ultimate pleasure, by enabling us to present your “progressive” ox power and cow protection as the economic base for the *varṇāśrama* system and the vegetarian, animal rights, and environmental solution to the modern age.

Your servants at The International Society for Cow Protection.

## ISKCON Foundation

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. As the years go by, your personality becomes more auspicious and amazing. The shelter of your lotus feet is the only relief from the blazing fire of material nature.

Since your departure, we have been struggling against so many obstacles and mischief, much of it of our own creation. Your leadership skills become more astounding as time passes. We can only hope to attain a fraction of your grace in order to carry on. Even your simple request, to maintain what you have left us, seems a daunting task at times. We are like a regiment whose leader has been slain on the battlefield, and we ourselves are falling over in an effort to maintain a defensive position.

By your grace, we have been given this rare opportunity to serve the pure devotee. We know that whatever we need is in your instructions. Here are the tools required to bring about a revolution in human consciousness; if we could only learn how to use them! Is it just a matter of extinguishing our petty defects?

We at the ISKCON Foundation hope that by sharing our experience in a common mission we may be able to better fulfill your desires. We are only praying for you to be present always with us so that we may never go astray in our intentions or purposes. You are the beacon that guides our humble effort.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we hear your call to grow and mature as the spiritual movement of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu expands. Our only hope is that whatever small advances have been made are pleasing to you. Please inspire us to continue in your footsteps. Your example is constantly showing us what is possible.

We remain your prostrated servants at the ISKCON Foundation.  
(written by Ambarīśa dāsa)

## ISKCON World Review

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, as many of your disciples did, I first perceived you as a lone hero who came at a particular time in history to present the all-encompassing, life-saving philosophy of Kṛṣṇa consciousness to a group of undeserving persons such as myself. We saw that you alone were able to counter the effects of Māyā on our miserable lives.

What we saw was just the surface of what you had to offer. While it is true that you are our hero, the greater reality is that you did not come to us alone.

You are actually the personal repository of all the good wishes and transcendental potency of all the great *ācāryas* who came before you. In cooperation with their desire to spread Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* movement, you selflessly carried Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya’s mercy everywhere:

*pṛthivīte āche yata nagarādi-grāma*  
*sarvatra pracāra haibe mora nāma*  
*\[CB Antya-khaṇḍa 4.126\]*

Your successes in doing this continue to be unlimited.

Your mercy and potency are like a Pacific mountain island, of which only the upper portion is visible to the eye. While these mountains may tower to the sky, much more of their substance lies beneath the surface of the ocean. So it is likewise true of the spiritual effect on the world’s population from your presence here. You are not like some historical character whose accomplishments read like a list of static facts. You are a dynamic spiritual personality presenting the conclusion of all the previous *ācāryas*, the effects of which continue to accrue new achievements by your disciples and granddisciples all around the world.

Your predecessor *ācāryas*, acting together for the pleasure of Lord Caitanya, armed you well, so that there is an unbreakable body of spiritual knowledge for us and many others yet to come. We at IWR have the opportunity to see how your followers are taking advantage of this gift by carrying out your instructions cooperatively. Wherever your sincere students go, you (as no other spiritual master has done) and all the *ācāryas* go with them to overwhelm the forces of illusion around the world.

I meditate on this every day. As time goes on, I can clearly see that you have not been acting alone but rather carrying this trust accumulated by all the *seva* of lifetimes of the great *ācāryas*. You have invested all this in us, like a family inheritance.

I also see that we are unable to utilize our inheritance properly unless we cooperate with each other. That is why we are reporting on the devotees who are carrying on your mission. By telling their stories, we can show that they are not acting alone but on the strength of this great gift you have given us, and in tandem with one another.

Please continue to help us take advantage of your association and the association of others that you have brought us. Please help us transcendentally cooperate and follow your instructions. Please help us serve this line of unlimitedly merciful spiritual masters that you so perfectly represent.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We fall in the dust of your lotus feet and pray to serve your movement.

The staff at ISKCON World Review.  
(written by Sarva-satya dāsa)

## KrishnaFest

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

The more we meditate on your glorious activities, the more we appreciate your unique qualities, abilities, and potencies. No Vaiṣṇava has been able to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness as widely as you. To single-handedly inaugurate a movement that is destined to save the world can only be performed by one possessed of uncommon transcendental power. We would like to humbly offer you the following song, as it expresses our appreciation of your unique position.

In order to expand your glories for the benefit of the devotees and conditioned souls alike, we plan to record this song and accompany it with a slide show of Your Divine Grace.

1\.  
Only you could lead us,  
Only you could touch our hearts,  
Only you could save us,  
Only you could play the part.

2\.  
Only you could know us,  
Only you could sympathize,  
Only you could help us,  
Only you could change our lives.

3\.  
Only you could hear us,  
Only you could understand,  
Only you could reach us,  
Only you could save our land.

Chorus

Only you would come here,  
Only you would take the chance,  
Only you would love us,  
Only you would make us dance.

Your servants at KrishnaFest.

## North American Centennial Committee

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet on your appearance day, the most auspicious day for the entire world.

As we celebrate the 97th anniversary of your appearance day, we are also preparing to celebrate the very special 100th anniversary in 1996. It may seem too early to begin planning for this event, but factually three years is barely enough time to prepare an adequate year-long celebration for you, the Founder-Ācārya of this great ISKCON mission. You are the greatest emissary of Lord Caitanya in the modern age. You have personally built the vehicle to deliver the entire modern world from its deeply sinful and hopeless condition. As your followers and students, we want to bring your message and your mercy to the entire world in a most significant and noteworthy way during your Centennial.

The Centennial celebration is providing a unique opportunity for all of your followers to unite for a most glorious and pleasing offering unto you.

We are trying to organize all of your followers from these categories:

- ISKCON Leaders
- Temple communities
- Temple congregations and Nama-hatta members
- Extended communities of your followers and disciples, and
- Indian communities.

Without everyone’s participation, the Centennial Celebration will not be complete. But real success will come only by creating a cooperative mood among all groups. Then by everyone’s cooperation we can please you the most and achieve a celebration that will awaken the world to your message.

We fall at your lotus feet and pray for your help and guidance. The task is so big and difficult. But you alone have always been able to attract everyone and encourage them. You have inspired all of your followers to dedicate themselves to your mission and to take up Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We pray that on this most auspicious day that you inspire all your followers to dedicate their energies together to glorify you on your Centennial.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have always encouraged everyone to serve Kṛṣṇa according to his or her propensity. You have encouraged our every desire to render devotional service, whether big or small. So, in the same way, please encourage everyone to step forward and, according to their desire and ability, render some special service on this most auspicious occasion.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have always asked everyone to please cooperate to push on the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. Thus we beg you, Please inspire all of us to cooperate to celebrate your Centennial anniversary.

Our plans are very expansive, but your Centennial Celebration will come only once in our lifetime. Together we can do great things to show your glories to the world. Individually, how much can we do? Actually, our individual efforts seem very insignificant and inadequate. So we can realize how very important it is to have everyone join together for this occasion. Please inspire us to more deeply dedicate ourselves to your Centennial on this day.

When there is a massive *kīrtana* in Māyāpur during the Gaura Pūrnimā festival, hundreds and hundreds of devotees gather. Some are intensely chanting in the center, and those on the periphery are just watching or casually listening. Then, as the *kīrtana* intensifies, everyone pulls in tightly. Finally, when the nectarean bliss explodes, everyone, even the most casual observer, becomes attracted and absorbed in the *kīrtana*. In the same way, please pull all of your followers in together, tighter and tighter. Then when the nectarean bliss of your Centennial Celebration explodes, even the most casual observer will be drawn in tightly at your lotus feet for this celebration.

Please bless us, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We feel totally incapable of rendering any service due to our impurities. With your mercy, all things are possible.

We beg to continue in your nectarean service.

Your servants on the North American Śrīla Prabhupāda Centennial Celebration Committee.  
(written by Ramīya dāsa)

## Padayātrā America

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to you, Lord Kṛṣṇa’s divine messenger, who appeared in this world for the good of all.

Your appearance in this world is indeed extraordinary. At an advanced age you crossed the ocean to come to us, outwardly appearing to be an unassuming Indian gentleman while inwardly carrying the spiritual potency that would change the lives of countless souls. Seeing your pure and compassionate heart, the Lord invested in you all divine potency for this difficult task. When we came in contact with you, this soul-stirring potency inspired, even in our proud and contaminated hearts, the desire of service and surrender to our eternal Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Being deeply affected by your unparalleled devotion and unswerving determination to give Kṛṣṇa to others, we have also become inspired to carry out this task as your menial servants. We have found this Padayātrā program to be very powerful for preaching and personal purification. The combined austerity and ecstasy of Padayātrā life helps cleanse the heart of the material disease as well as give a very sweet taste of spiritual life. It is also a most congenial and effective means to draw out the dormant spiritual inquisitiveness and service attitude of multitudes of sleeping *jīvas*. Everyone is charmed to see the oxen, forgotten symbol of religiosity, pulling the most beautiful forms of Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurasundara, and accompanied by the *kīrtana* of the holy name. People very much like this preaching pilgrimage, which is a pure, simple, and straightforward presentation of devotional life.

We would like to report that we have recently completed a one-and-half-year, 1,700-mile walk covering seven Central American countries, starting in Belize and ending in Panama. Many thought this walk impossible or dangerous because of guerrilla activity and unfavorable political situations. But because we had some faith in your desire to present Kṛṣṇa consciousness everywhere, in the potency of Padayātrā, in the potency of the holy name, and in Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s famous every-town-and-village prediction, somehow it has come out successfully.

We want to thank you for engaging us in this wonderful program, under the inspired leadership of your dedicated disciple His Holiness Lokanāth Swami. We beg you to please overlook our faults and continue to give us some little service to do for your pleasure, eternally.

Your humble servants in Padayātrā America.

## Padayātrā India

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

“My lord, devotees like your good self are verily holy places personified. Because you carry the Personality of Godhead within your heart, you turn all places into places of pilgrimage” (*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 1.13.10). When Caitanya Mahāprabhu met Sanātana Gosvāmī in Vārāṇasī, Sanātana appeared like a Muslim mendicant since he was serving and associating with the Nawab Hussain Shah. The Lord was overwhelmed with ecstatic love and embraced Sanātana. Then the Lord began cleansing Sanātana Gosvāmī’s body, saying, “I am touching you just to purify Myself, because by the force of your devotional service you can purify the whole universe.”

Similarly you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, entered our lives. When we appeared in so many ridiculous dresses due to serving the various energies /agencies of Māyā, you so patiently and so lovingly came to us, served us, and showed us the proper path of returning home, back to Godhead, by the devotional force of *bhakti-yoga*. When the Lord’s unalloyed devotee Vidura returned from visiting the sacred places of pilgrimage, Mahārāja Yudhiṣṭhira received his uncle, saying, “You yourself are a holy place because you are an advanced devotee. People like you always carry Lord Viṣṇu in their hearts. You can revitalize all holy places after they have been polluted by the pilgrimages of sinners.”

Similarly you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, have been visiting all the holy places on your *saṅkīrtana* Padayātrā for the past nine years, traveling some 35,000 km. with Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurasundara deep within your heart. Your presence has been unbelievable—inside the holy *tīrthas,* outside, everywhere and anywhere you appear. Not only are the pilgrims’ sins counteracted and the *tīrthas* purified by your visits, but all the other fortunate conditioned souls who have your *darśana* day and night, throughout the years—on the roadsides, from their shops, cars, buses, trucks, rickshas, cycles, *tongas,* bullock carts, and deep inside the villages, where you always desired to preach. Since you are the most dear loving servant and the most advanced devotee of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, the conditioned souls are automatically on pilgrimage, because wherever you are with Nitāi-Gaurasundara, there is a holy place in itself. Day by day, you and Their Lordships are manifesting holy *tīrthas* in every village, town, and city throughout this land, and in other countries as well!

Narottama dāsa Ṭhākura says, t*īrtha-yātrā pariśrama, kevala mānera bhrama:* “Visiting holy places is simply another type of bewilderment.” This is our illusion, thinking we are so fortunate to visit so many holy places, but actually, it’s simply a waste of time! Only by your intimate association on Padayātrā, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are we beginning to realize the importance of your appearance here: to purify every space and place on this planet with your transcendental walking steps!!!

Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*!

Your most insignificant servants, the walking padayātrīs in India (including the four-legged boys!)  
(written by Jaya Vijaya dāsa)

## Padayātrā Europe

**Belfast to Moscow (1990-1996)**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

We have slid, swam, walked, and flown all over this universe, with unimaginable bodies, through unimaginable depths of ignorance. But like unlimited zeroes without a one in front, all our activities, although varied, didn’t help us progress an inch—until, that is, they were engaged in following you and ISKCON.

Often when we are on the road, the most enlivening realization that we share together is that of your direct mercy. There is often absolutely no other shelter but you, as, time after time, our careful planning collapses around us.

You have shown us real material freedom by leading us to abandon all our material mental calculations of what is or is not possible. Please give us the intelligence to have fixed remembrance of you as our protector, benefactor, and ever-loving well-wisher.

Aspiring to serve you sincerely,  
Your granddisicples on Pada-yātrā Europe.

## Radio Krishna Centrale

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Most dear and beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my most humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet again and again.

Your lotus feet are always dancing on the transcendental land of Vṛndavāna Dhāma, and their glories are spread all over the three worlds. They are perfectly capable, and in themselves sufficient, to give complete shelter to all spirit souls.

Such is their power of purification that, after having been in contact with them willingly, unwillingly, or unknowingly, even for a fraction of a moment, one is forcefully dragged to reach perfection, no matter how deeply one is sunk into the material atmosphere. Remembering your wonderful personality, your teachings, your instructions, and acting accordingly, any living entity can resume his topmost fortunate position as a loving servant of the Supreme Lord and His pure devotees.

You have been teaching us that, inconceivably deciding to follow our desire to substitute ourselves for the Supreme Personality of Godhead and thus misusing our infinitesimal independence, we have been given unto the care of Durgā-devī. By putting us under the spell of her deluding power, she shows us what happens if we go against our nature of energy (servants) and behave as if we were the energetic (master) and thus try to enjoy this unlimited manifestation through our senses. By sinking us under the cloud of ignorance, she makes us forget who is really the Supreme Lord, and by forgetting this we also fail to remember which particular, eternal, loving relationship ties us to the Supreme Personality of Godhead and His devotees. In this way, losing the ability to identify ourselves with our original eternal personality, we forget our particular identity. Just like the sunlight’s photons appear as if they exist separately from the sun globe when filtered through a rain cloud, so under the cloud of ignorance we imagine that we exist separate from the Supreme Personality of Godhead and that we are independent, just like Him.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, due to this forgetfulness we have been loitering in this dark and dangerous material universe, completely lost and in search of a permanent, eternal platform from which to imitate the Supreme Controller. As a result, we have become the very surrendered servants of the most obnoxious, perverted desires, worse than street dogs, experiencing a continuous, never-ending stream of suffering.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, by putting us back into our original condition you have showered, over and over, your unlimited mercy upon us, even though we do not at all deserve such a matchless gift. Please, by your divine association, give us a fraction of the pure love that you have for Śrī Kṛṣṇa so that the same love, dormant in our hearts, can be reawakened; just as the fire dormant inside wood can be reawakened by contacting a spark.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, personally I’m only an imitation devotee, and not even a very good imitation, and yet you are the owner of my life and I belong to you. I am completely confident that if I keep trying again and again to follow your instructions, the moment will come when my heart will be cleansed from every dirty desire and will be a suitable place for Your Divine Grace to sit.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, if you keep allowing me to stay in your association, even if I’m a fool and rascal, I’m sure that I’ll be able to reach the point where my only reason to act will be to please Your Divine Grace.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, I take this opportunity to again renew my prayer: Please never let me forget your wonderful personality, pastimes, and instructions so that wherever I may be, I’ll always be with Your Divine Grace. Hare Kṛṣṇa!

Your servants at Radio Krishna Centrale, Terni, Italy.  
(writtenby Tridaṇḍī dāsa)

## Surabhi-kuñja

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances and my attempt of an offering to you on this most important day.

On behalf of the Surabhi-kuñja devotees, I hope you will be pleased by a brief description of our new community, our goals to serve you, and a prayer for strength and guidance.

Surabhi-kuñj is a rural farm community located in Efland, North Carolina, just twenty miles from the University of North Carolina and Duke University. The farm village is five miles from our transcendental neighbor, New Goloka, and is the home of the International Society For Cow Protection. The community consists of 125 acres divided into fourteen plots of land. Thirteen plots are owned individually by your disciples and granddisciples, and one twelve-acre plot is owned collectively for community projects such as a temple, school, and park. This project is also extended beyond the land to the greater community of devotees living in the area.

The devotees in Surabhi Kuñj are working on various projects and settings goals as their devotional service. These projects and goals are being accomplished either by individuals or collectively. I would like to list these as our offering to you:

1\. To always encourage each other to follow your basic and most important instruction of hearing and chanting the holy names of Kṛṣṇa by having a regular morning program, observing festivals, and reading and discussing your books.

2\. To have our own Kṛṣṇa conscious school so our children can be taught by devotees, thereby encouraging them in their Kṛṣṇa consciousness while they learn academics.

3\. To make a contribution to fulfill your desire of starting *varṇāśrama* by each of us earning an honorable livelihood and contributing to support *brāhmaṇas* within our community for teaching, preaching, etc.

4\. To gradually develop more agricultural, self-sufficiency, and *prasādam* distribution programs.

5\. To create an atmosphere that encourages both individual achievement and collective cooperation to enable people of different temperaments—preachers, teachers, farmers, businessmen, laborers, etc.—to work together and respect one another’s services.

6\. To present the community as an example in preaching at the three major local universities, so that visitors will come and associate with devotees.

In an attempt to fulfill these goals, I would like to say a prayer to help me work in the mood of cooperation:

You have not only translated the Vedic scriptures and brought them to the English-speaking countries to fulfill the order of your spiritual master, but most importantly you have lived these translations. And of all the instructions you have carefully handed down to us, the following stands out in my mind. I repeat this instruction, desiring your mercy, to understand its real meaning:

*tṛṇād api sunīcena taror iva sahiṣṇunā*  
*amāninā mānadena kīrtanīya sadā hariḥ*

**“One should chant the holy name of the Lord in a humble state of mind, thinking oneself lower than the straw in the street; one should be more tolerant than a tree, devoid of all sense of false prestige, and should be ready to offer all respects to others. In such a state of mind, one can chant the holy name of the Lord constantly.”**

You have stated in so many places—in your books, your morning walks, your lectures—”Somehow or another just develop an attraction to hear about Kṛṣṇa.” Following this instruction can solve any problem and make any project a success. Please help me to control the mind, cooperate with my Godbrothers, and sincerely chant Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare.

Your servants at the Surabhi-kuñja community, Efland, North Carolina.  
(written by Śuddha-jīva dāsa)

## Vaiṣṇava Institute for Higher Education (VIHE)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

A devotee recently explained in *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* class that your achievements are huge like the highest mountain and your Kṛṣṇa consciousness as deep as the deepest ocean.

The first part is easy to understand. Your accomplishments are there for the world to see. They are obvious. Just as we can’t miss seeing a mountain in front of us, we can’t help being awed by your unparalleled feats in preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Your books, temples, and devotees are there for anyone to see.

Your depth of Kṛṣṇa consciousness is another thing. The class-giver explained that it isn’t so easy to understand the depth of the ocean. Indeed, in some places it’s deeper than the mountain is high, but we wouldn’t know that from the surface. Only with the proper instruments can one judge its depth. In the case of *mahā-bhāgavatas* like you, it “takes one to know one.” Unfortunately, I’m not “one.” I deal mainly with externals, and have little capacity for spiritual subtleties, being governed mainly by the mode of passion.

I have found, however, a loophole—a peek into some beginning of a beginning of this appreciation. It’s the association of those with appreciation. At the VIHE we have the good fortune to host many of your disciples. Their appreciation, love, and dedication to you rubs off on those they contact. One day Girirāja Swami tells us about the Bombay struggle, the next day Jayādvaita Swami repeats instructions he received from you about editing your books. Later, Satsvarūpa Mahārāja recalls the early days at 26 Second Ave. These are regular occurrences in Vṛndavāna, especially in Kārttika, when so many come to honor your disappearance day. Thank you for the chance to learn about you in this way. Thank you for giving me a service in your mission. Thank you for the chance to serve in Śrī Vṛndavāna Dhāma.

Your servants at the Vaiṣṇava Institute for Higher Education.  
(written by Braja-bihārī dāsa)
